  window.saveNavigatorPassPlanSettings=async function(){
    const plan=ppLoadPlan();
    PLAN_GRADES.forEach(g=>{
      const en=document.getElementById(`pp-enable-${g}`),date=document.getElementById(`pp-date-${g}`);
      plan.grades[g].enabled=!!(en&&en.checked);plan.grades[g].examDate=date?date.value:'';
      const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll(`input[data-pp-subject="${g}"]`)];
      plan.grades[g].subjects=boxes.filter(b=>b.checked).map(b=>b.value);
    });
    const cap=document.getElementById('pp-daily-cap'),finalDays=document.getElementById('pp-final-days');
    plan.dailyCap=Math.max(40,Math.min(250,Number(cap&&cap.value)||120));
    plan.finalDays=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,Number(finalDays&&finalDays.value)||4));
    const today=ppDateKey(new Date());
    const invalid=PLAN_GRADES.filter(g=>plan.grades[g].enabled&&(!plan.grades[g].examDate||ppDiffDays(today,plan.grades[g].examDate)<0));
    if(invalid.length){showToast('활성화한 급수의 시험일을 오늘 이후 날짜로 설정하세요.');return}
    if(PLAN_GRADES.some(g=>plan.grades[g].enabled&&(!plan.grades[g].subjects||!plan.grades[g].subjects.length))){showToast('활성화한 급수에서 최소 1개 과목을 선택하세요.');return}
    ppSavePlan(plan);showToast('합격 플랜 설정을 저장했습니다.');renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
  };
  window.recalculateNavigatorPassPlanToday=function(){
    const daily=ppLoadDaily(),today=ppDateKey(new Date());delete daily[today];ppSaveDaily(daily);renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
  };
  window.resetNavigatorPassPlanProgress=function(){
    showConfirm('2·3급 합격 플랜의 학습 진도와 오늘 숙제를 모두 초기화하시겠습니까?\n기존 일반 오답노트와 기출 풀이 기록은 유지됩니다.',()=>{
      safeStorageSet(PROGRESS_KEY,'{}','합격 플랜 진도');safeStorageSet(DAILY_KEY,'{}','합격 플랜 오늘 숙제');renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
    });
  };

  function ppFindQuestion(gradeId,item){
    for(const hit of item.hits){
      const exam=getPastExam(gradeId,hit.year,hit.session);if(!exam)continue;
      const q=exam.questions.find(q=>q['과목']===item.subject&&Number(q['번호'])===Number(hit.number)&&n3aNormalizeQuestion(q['문제'])===item.normalized);
      if(q)return {...q,_year:hit.year,_session:hit.session,'회차':hit.session,_short:gradeId,_planKey:item.key,_planGrade:gradeId,_planCount:item.count,_planGroupId:item.groupId};
    }
    return null;
  }
  async function ppHydrateKeys(keys){
    const grades=[...new Set(keys.map(k=>(ppGetItemByKey(k)||{}).gradeId).filter(Boolean))];
    renderPastLoading('오늘 숙제 문제를 준비하는 중입니다');
    for(const g of grades){if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(g))await ensurePastDataForSubject(g)}
    const out=[];
    keys.forEach(key=>{const item=ppGetItemByKey(key);if(!item)return;const q=ppFindQuestion(item.gradeId,item);if(q)out.push(q)});
    await ensurePastExplainForYears(out.map(q=>q._year));
    return out;
  }
  window.startNavigatorPassPlanMock=async function(gradeId){
    if(!PLAN_GRADES.includes(gradeId))return;
    const plan=ppLoadPlan(),data=n3aData(gradeId)||await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(gradeId);
    const subjects=ppSelectedSubjects(plan,gradeId,data);
    renderPastLoading(`${GRADE_LABELS[gradeId]} 2026 실전 모의를 준비하는 중입니다`);
    try{
      if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(gradeId))await ensurePastDataForSubject(gradeId);
      const exams=getPastExamsByBase(gradeId,'상선').filter(p=>p.meta&&Number(p.meta.year)===2026).sort((a,b)=>Number(b.meta.session)-Number(a.meta.session));
      if(!exams.length)throw new Error('2026년 상선 기출 회차가 없습니다.');
      const latest=exams[0];
      pastSubjectId=gradeId;pastBaseShort=gradeId;pastYear=Number(latest.meta.year);pastSession=Number(latest.meta.session);pastVariant='상선';pastAllYears=false;pastReturnView=null;
      await startPastSession('mock',subjects);
    }catch(e){
      app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px"><b>실전 모의를 시작하지 못했습니다.</b><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:7px">${escapeHtml(e.message||String(e))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="renderNavigatorPassPlan('${planEntrySubject}')">합격 플랜으로 돌아가기</button></div>`;
    }
  };

  window.startNavigatorPassPlanToday=async function(){
    const plan=ppLoadPlan();await ppBuildPools(plan);const progress=ppLoadProgress(),today=ppDateKey(new Date());
    const assignment=await ppGetDailyAssignment(plan,planPools,progress,false);
    const allKeys=(assignment.keys||[]).slice();
    const unresolvedKeys=allKeys.filter(k=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,k),today));
    if(!unresolvedKeys.length){showToast('오늘 숙제를 모두 완료했습니다.');ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);return}
    try{
      const checkpoint=ppLoadPassSessionCheckpoint();
      const queue=await ppHydrateKeys(allKeys);if(!queue.length||queue.length!==allKeys.length)throw new Error('문제 원문을 찾지 못했습니다.');
      const checkpointMatches=checkpoint&&Array.isArray(checkpoint.queueKeys)&&checkpoint.queueKeys.length===allKeys.length&&checkpoint.queueKeys.every((k,i)=>k===allKeys[i]);
      if(checkpointMatches){
        const nextIndex=Number(checkpoint.nextIndex);
        if(Number.isInteger(nextIndex)&&nextIndex>=0&&nextIndex<queue.length){
          planSessionQueue=queue;planSessionIdx=nextIndex;
          planSessionAnswers=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionConfidence=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);
          if(Array.isArray(checkpoint.answers))checkpoint.answers.slice(0,queue.length).forEach((v,i)=>{if(v===null||Number.isInteger(v))planSessionAnswers[i]=v});
          if(Array.isArray(checkpoint.confidence))checkpoint.confidence.slice(0,queue.length).forEach((v,i)=>{if(v===null||['sure','unsure','wrong'].includes(v))planSessionConfidence[i]=v});
          planSessionCommitted=new Set(Array.isArray(checkpoint.committed)?checkpoint.committed.filter(i=>Number.isInteger(i)&&i>=0&&i<queue.length):[]);
          for(let i=0;i<nextIndex;i++)if(planSessionAnswers[i]!==null)planSessionCommitted.add(i);
          planSessionStartedAt=Date.now();currentMode='pass-plan-session';renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();return;
        }
      }
      if(checkpoint)ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();
      let startIndex=allKeys.findIndex(k=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,k),today));
      if(startIndex<0)startIndex=0;
      planSessionQueue=queue;planSessionIdx=startIndex;planSessionAnswers=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionConfidence=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionStartedAt=Date.now();planSessionCommitted=new Set();
      for(let i=0;i<startIndex;i++)if(ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,allKeys[i]),today))planSessionCommitted.add(i);
      currentMode='pass-plan-session';ppSavePassSessionCheckpoint(startIndex);renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
    }catch(e){app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px"><b>오늘 숙제를 시작하지 못했습니다.</b><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:7px">${escapeHtml(e.message||String(e))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="renderNavigatorPassPlan('${planEntrySubject}')">플랜으로 돌아가기</button></div>`}
  };
  function ppGradeColor(g){return g==='navi2'?'#2563EB':'#059669'}
  window.renderNavigatorPassPlanCard=function(){
    if(currentMode!=='pass-plan-session')return;
    if(planSessionIdx>=planSessionQueue.length){renderNavigatorPassPlanResult();return}
    const q=planSessionQueue[planSessionIdx],selected=planSessionAnswers[planSessionIdx],confidence=planSessionConfidence[planSessionIdx],correct=q['정답'],showFeedback=selected!==null;
    const grade=q._planGrade,color=ppGradeColor(grade),markers=['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
    const isCorrect=showFeedback&&selected===correct;
    const canNext=showFeedback;
    app.innerHTML=`

  window.chooseNavigatorPassPlanAnswer=function(i){
    if(currentMode!=='pass-plan-session')return;
    const q=planSessionQueue[planSessionIdx];
    if(planSessionAnswers[planSessionIdx]!==null||!q||i<0||i>=q['선택지'].length)return;
    const confidence=i===q['정답']?'sure':'wrong';
    planSessionAnswers[planSessionIdx]=i;planSessionConfidence[planSessionIdx]=confidence;
    if(!planSessionCommitted.has(planSessionIdx)){ppCommitOutcome(q,i,confidence);planSessionCommitted.add(planSessionIdx)}
    ppSavePassSessionCheckpoint(planSessionIdx+1);
    renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
  };

  function ppCommitOutcome(q,answer,confidence){

      const retainedAcrossDay=!!(priorConfirmed&&priorConfirmed<today);

        removePastWrong(pqid(q));
      }else if(priorF

  window.saveNavigatorPassPlanSettings=async function(){
    const plan=ppLoadPlan();
    PLAN_GRADES.forEach(g=>{
      const en=document.getElementById(`pp-enable-${g}`),date=document.getElementById(`pp-date-${g}`);
      plan.grades[g].enabled=!!(en&&en.checked);plan.grades[g].examDate=date?date.value:'';
      const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll(`input[data-pp-subject="${g}"]`)];
      plan.grades[g].subjects=boxes.filter(b=>b.checked).map(b=>b.value);
    });
    const cap=document.getElementById('pp-daily-cap'),finalDays=document.getElementById('pp-final-days');
    plan.dailyCap=Math.max(40,Math.min(250,Number(cap&&cap.value)||120));
    plan.finalDays=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,Number(finalDays&&finalDays.value)||4));
    const today=ppDateKey(new Date());
    const invalid=PLAN_GRADES.filter(g=>plan.grades[g].enabled&&(!plan.grades[g].examDate||ppDiffDays(today,plan.grades[g].examDate)<0));
    if(invalid.length){showToast('활성화한 급수의 시험일을 오늘 이후 날짜로 설정하세요.');return}
    if(PLAN_GRADES.some(g=>plan.grades[g].enabled&&(!plan.grades[g].subjects||!plan.grades[g].subjects.length))){showToast('활성화한 급수에서 최소 1개 과목을 선택하세요.');return}
    ppSavePlan(plan);showToast('합격 플랜 설정을 저장했습니다.');renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
  };
  window.recalculateNavigatorPassPlanToday=function(){
    const daily=ppLoadDaily(),today=ppDateKey(new Date());delete daily[today];ppSaveDaily(daily);renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
  };
  window.resetNavigatorPassPlanProgress=function(){
    showConfirm('2·3급 합격 플랜의 학습 진도와 오늘 숙제를 모두 초기화하시겠습니까?\n기존 일반 오답노트와 기출 풀이 기록은 유지됩니다.',()=>{
      safeStorageSet(PROGRESS_KEY,'{}','합격 플랜 진도');safeStorageSet(DAILY_KEY,'{}','합격 플랜 오늘 숙제');renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);
    });
  };

  function ppFindQuestion(gradeId,item){
    for(const hit of item.hits){
      const exam=getPastExam(gradeId,hit.year,hit.session);if(!exam)continue;
      const q=exam.questions.find(q=>q['과목']===item.subject&&Number(q['번호'])===Number(hit.number)&&n3aNormalizeQuestion(q['문제'])===item.normalized);
      if(q)return {...q,_year:hit.year,_session:hit.session,'회차':hit.session,_short:gradeId,_planKey:item.key,_planGrade:gradeId,_planCount:item.count,_planGroupId:item.groupId};
    }
    return null;
  }
  async function ppHydrateKeys(keys){
    const grades=[...new Set(keys.map(k=>(ppGetItemByKey(k)||{}).gradeId).filter(Boolean))];
    renderPastLoading('오늘 숙제 문제를 준비하는 중입니다');
    for(const g of grades){if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(g))await ensurePastDataForSubject(g)}
    const out=[];
    keys.forEach(key=>{const item=ppGetItemByKey(key);if(!item)return;const q=ppFindQuestion(item.gradeId,item);if(q)out.push(q)});
    await ensurePastExplainForYears(out.map(q=>q._year));
    return out;
  }
  window.startNavigatorPassPlanMock=async function(gradeId){
    if(!PLAN_GRADES.includes(gradeId))return;
    const plan=ppLoadPlan(),data=n3aData(gradeId)||await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(gradeId);
    const subjects=ppSelectedSubjects(plan,gradeId,data);
    renderPastLoading(`${GRADE_LABELS[gradeId]} 2026 실전 모의를 준비하는 중입니다`);
    try{
      if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(gradeId))await ensurePastDataForSubject(gradeId);
      const exams=getPastExamsByBase(gradeId,'상선').filter(p=>p.meta&&Number(p.meta.year)===2026).sort((a,b)=>Number(b.meta.session)-Number(a.meta.session));
      if(!exams.length)throw new Error('2026년 상선 기출 회차가 없습니다.');
      const latest=exams[0];
      pastSubjectId=gradeId;pastBaseShort=gradeId;pastYear=Number(latest.meta.year);pastSession=Number(latest.meta.session);pastVariant='상선';pastAllYears=false;pastReturnView=null;
      await startPastSession('mock',subjects);
    }catch(e){
      app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px"><b>실전 모의를 시작하지 못했습니다.</b><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:7px">${escapeHtml(e.message||String(e))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="renderNavigatorPassPlan('${planEntrySubject}')">합격 플랜으로 돌아가기</button></div>`;
    }
  };

  window.startNavigatorPassPlanToday=async function(){
    const plan=ppLoadPlan();await ppBuildPools(plan);const progress=ppLoadProgress(),today=ppDateKey(new Date());
    const assignment=await ppGetDailyAssignment(plan,planPools,progress,false);
    const allKeys=(assignment.keys||[]).slice();
    const unresolvedKeys=allKeys.filter(k=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,k),today));
    if(!unresolvedKeys.length){showToast('오늘 숙제를 모두 완료했습니다.');ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);return}
    try{
      const checkpoint=ppLoadPassSessionCheckpoint();
      const queue=await ppHydrateKeys(allKeys);if(!queue.length||queue.length!==allKeys.length)throw new Error('문제 원문을 찾지 못했습니다.');
      const checkpointMatches=checkpoint&&Array.isArray(checkpoint.queueKeys)&&checkpoint.queueKeys.length===allKeys.length&&checkpoint.queueKeys.every((k,i)=>k===allKeys[i]);
      if(checkpointMatches){
        const nextIndex=Number(checkpoint.nextIndex);
        if(Number.isInteger(nextIndex)&&nextIndex>=0&&nextIndex<queue.length){
          planSessionQueue=queue;planSessionIdx=nextIndex;
          planSessionAnswers=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionConfidence=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);
          if(Array.isArray(checkpoint.answers))checkpoint.answers.slice(0,queue.length).forEach((v,i)=>{if(v===null||Number.isInteger(v))planSessionAnswers[i]=v});
          if(Array.isArray(checkpoint.confidence))checkpoint.confidence.slice(0,queue.length).forEach((v,i)=>{if(v===null||['sure','unsure','wrong'].includes(v))planSessionConfidence[i]=v});
          planSessionCommitted=new Set(Array.isArray(checkpoint.committed)?checkpoint.committed.filter(i=>Number.isInteger(i)&&i>=0&&i<queue.length):[]);
          for(let i=0;i<nextIndex;i++)if(planSessionAnswers[i]!==null)planSessionCommitted.add(i);
          planSessionStartedAt=Date.now();currentMode='pass-plan-session';renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();return;
        }
      }
      if(checkpoint)ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();
      let startIndex=allKeys.findIndex(k=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,k),today));
      if(startIndex<0)startIndex=0;
      planSessionQueue=queue;planSessionIdx=startIndex;planSessionAnswers=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionConfidence=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionStartedAt=Date.now();planSessionCommitted=new Set();
      for(let i=0;i<startIndex;i++)if(ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,allKeys[i]),today))planSessionCommitted.add(i);
      currentMode='pass-plan-session';ppSavePassSessionCheckpoint(startIndex);renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
    }catch(e){app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px"><b>오늘 숙제를 시작하지 못했습니다.</b><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:7px">${escapeHtml(e.message||String(e))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="renderNavigatorPassPlan('${planEntrySubject}')">플랜으로 돌아가기</button></div>`}
  };

        // A question confirmed on an earlier date and recalled again today is mastered.
        r.status='mastered';r.mastered=true;r.masteryReviews=(r.masteryReviews||0)+1;
        r.sameDayFirstCorrectDate=today;r.sameDayConfirmedDate=today;r.recoveryStartDate=null;
        const exam=ppExamDay(ppLoadPlan(),q._planGrade);let due=ppAddDays(today,3);if(exam){const dayBefore=ppAddDays(exam,-1);due=ppMinDate(due,dayBefore)}r.dueDate=due;
        removePastWrong(pqid(q));
      }else if(priorFirstCorrect===today){
        // Second correct recall on the same day: confirmed for today, but not mastered yet.
        if(!r.firstPassDate)r.firstPassDate=today;
        r.status='provisional';r.mastered=false;r.sameDayFirstCorrectDate=today;r.sameDayConfirmedDate=today;r.recoveryStartDate=null;r.dueDate=ppAddDays(today,1);
        removePastWrong(pqid(q));
      }else{
        // First correct recall of the day. Keep it unresolved so a delayed same-day check is required.
        if(!r.firstPassDate)r.firstPassDate=today;
        r.status='provisional';r.mastered=false;r.sameDayFirstCorrectDate=today;r.sameDayConfirmedDate=null;r.dueDate=today;
      }
    }else{
      r.status='weak';r.mastered=false;r.recoveryStartDate=today;r.dueDate=today;
      r.sameDayFirstCorrectDate=null;r.sameDayConfirmedDate=null;
      if(!correct)addPastWrong(q,answer);
    }
    progress[q._planKey]=r;ppSaveProgress(progress);
  }
  window.prevNavigatorPassPlanQuestion=function(){
    if(currentMode!=='pass-plan-session'||planSessionIdx<=0)return;
    planSessionIdx--;
    ppSavePassSessionCheckpoint(planSessionIdx);
    renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
  };
  window.nextNavigatorPassPlanQuestion=function(){
    if(currentMode!=='pass-plan-session')return;const q=planSessionQueue[planSessionIdx],answer=planSessionAnswers[planSessionIdx],conf=planSessionConfidence[planSessionIdx];if(answer===null)return;
    if(!planSessionCommitted.has(planSessionIdx)){ppCommitOutcome(q,answer,conf||'wrong');planSessionCommitted.add(planSessionIdx)}
    planSessionIdx++;
    if(planSessionIdx>=planSessionQueue.length)ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();else ppSavePassSessionCheckpoint(planSessionIdx);
    renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
  };
  window.renderNavigatorPassPlanResult=function(){
    currentMode='pass-plan-result';const today=ppDateKey(new Date()),progress=ppLoadProgress();let sure=0,unsure=0,wrong=0;
    planSessionQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{const a=planSessionAnswers[i],c=planSessionConfidence[i];if(a===q['정답']&&c==='sure')sure++;else if(a===q['정답'])unsure++;else wrong++});
    const unresolved=planSessionQueue.filter(q=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,q._planKey),today));const elapsed=Math.floor((Date.now()-planSessionStartedAt)/1000);
    app.innerHTML=`<div style="padding-top:22px;margin-bottom:14px"><div style="font-size:12px;letter-spacing:2px;font-weight:900;color:#7C3AED">TODAY RESULT</div><h1 style="font-size:24px;margin:4px 0">오늘의 숙제 결과</h1></div><div class="card" style="text-align:center;border-left:4px solid #7C3AED"><div style="font-size:40px;font-weight:900;color:#7C3AED">${sure} / ${planSessionQueue.length}</div><div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;margin-top:5px">정답 + 확실 통과</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-top:5px">애매 ${unsure} · 오답 ${wrong} · ${Math.floor(elapsed/60)}분 ${elapsed%60}초</div></div>${unresolved.length?`<div class="card" style="background:#FEF3C7;border-left:4px solid #D97706"><div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#92400E">미해결 ${unresolved.length}문제</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:#78350F;margin-top:5px;line-height:1.6">오늘 숙제 완료로 처리되지 않습니다. 같은 날 다시 맞혀 ‘확실’까지 만들 수 있지만, 숙달 판정은 다른 날 재확인이 필요합니다.</div></div><button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;background:#D97706;border-color:#D97706" onclick="retryNavigatorPassPlanUnresolved()">미해결만 다시 풀기 · ${unresolved.length}문제</button>`:`<div class="card" style="background:#D1FAE5;text-align:center"><div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#065F46">오늘 숙제 완료</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:#047857;margin-top:5px">내일 복습 대상은 시험일까지 자동으로 다시 계산됩니다.</div></div>`}<button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" onclick="renderNavigatorPassPlan('${planEntrySubject}')">합격 플랜으로 돌아가기</button>`;
  };
  window.retryNavigatorPassPlanUnresolved=async function(){
    const today=ppDateKey(new Date()),progress=ppLoadProgress();const keys=planSessionQueue.map(q=>q._planKey).filter(k=>!ppTodayCleared(ppProgressFor(progress,k),today));if(!keys.length){renderNavigatorPassPlan(planEntrySubject);return}
    const queue=await ppHydrateKeys(keys);ppClearPassSessionCheckpoint();planSessionQueue=shuffle(queue);planSessionIdx=0;planSessionAnswers=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionConfidence=new Array(queue.length).fill(null);planSessionStartedAt=Date.now();planSessionCommitted=new Set();currentMode='pass-plan-session';renderNavigatorPassPlanCard();
  };

  // 첫 진입 화면이 이미 렌더된 뒤 이 스크립트가 로드되므로 이후 내비게이션부터 카드가 표시됩니다.
})();

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]!==null)return;
  pastAnswers[pastIdx] = i;
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastNext(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]===null)return;
  if(pastIdx+1 >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  pastIdx++;
  renderPastCard();
}

function renderPastResult(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  currentMode='past-result';
  clearPastProgress();
  const total = pastQueue.length;
  const isNavi3Practice = pastReturnView === 'navi3-practice';
  const analysisGradeLabel = ((n3aData()&&n3aData().meta&&n3aData().meta.gradeLabel)||'항해사').replace(/\s*\(상선\)\s*/g,'');
  let correct = 0;
  const wrongs = [];
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) correct++;
    else wrongs.push({q, i, ans: pastAnswers[i]});
  });
  const pct = total ? Math.round(100*correct/total) : 0;

    </div>
  `;
}

function escapeHtml(s){
  return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c){
    return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[c];
  });
}


const getImp=id=>IMP[id]||3;
const stars=n=>icon('star',12).repeat(n);
const BOX_INT={1:1,2:3,3:7};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

// v4.51: SM-2 기반 개인화 간격 반복
// 각 문제마다 난이도(ef)/다음 복습 간격(interval)/연속 정답(reps)/다음 복습일(nextReview) 추적
// Leitner 박스는 UI/통계 지표로 그대로 유지, 스케줄은 SM-2 interval이 주도
const SM2_DEFAULT_EF = 2.5;
const SM2_MIN_EF = 1.3;

// SM-2 품질 점수 매핑 (이 앱의 3버튼 입력):
// ○ 맞음(sure)   -> q=5 (perfect)
// △ 애매(unsure) -> q=3 (correct with difficulty)
// ✕ 틀림         -> q=1 (incorrect)
function qualityFromInput(correct, confidence){
  if(!correct) return 1;
  if(confidence==='sure') return 5;
  return 3;
}

// 순수 SM-2 계산. item을 변이시키고 nextReview(ISO) 갱신.
function updateSM2(item, q){
  // EF 갱신 (q에 무관하게 항상 적용되는 게 원안)
  let ef = (typeof item.ef==='number') ? item.ef : SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  ef = ef + (0.1 - (5-q)*(0.08 + (5-q)*0.02));
  if(ef < SM2_MIN_EF) ef = SM2_MIN_EF;
  item.ef = Math.round(ef*1000)/1000;

  let reps = item.reps || 0;
  let interval;

  if(q < 3){
    // 실패 → 처음부터
    reps = 0;
    interval = 1;
  } else {
    if(reps === 0) interval = 1;
    else if(reps === 1) interval = 6;
    else interval = Math.round((item.interval||1) * item.ef);
    reps += 1;
  }

  item.reps = reps;
  item.interval = interval;
  const next = new Date();
  next.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  next.setDate(next.getDate() + interval);
  item.nextReview = next.toISOString();
}

// v4.83: 같은 날의 반복은 숙달 연습으로만 취급하고 간격복습 횟수는 하루 1회만 반영합니다.
// 같은 날 평가가 여러 개면 가장 낮은 품질을 사용해 과도한 복습 연장을 막습니다.
function studyDateKey(){return toLocalDateInput(new Date())}
function updateSpacedReview(item,q){
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.sm2Day!==today){
    item.sm2Day=today;
    item.sm2DayQuality=q;
    item.sm2DayBase={ef:item.ef,reps:item.reps,interval:item.interval,nextReview:item.nextReview};
    updateSM2(item,q);
    return;
  }
  const dailyQ=Math.min(typeof item.sm2DayQuality==='number'?item.sm2DayQuality:5,q);
  if(dailyQ===item.sm2DayQuality)return;
  const base=item.sm2DayBase||{};
  item.ef=typeof base.ef==='number'?base.ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  item.reps=typeof base.reps==='number'?base.reps:0;
  item.interval=typeof base.interval==='number'?base.interval:0;
  item.nextReview=base.nextReview||null;
  item.sm2DayQuality=dailyQ;
  updateSM2(item,dailyQ);
}

function shuffle(a){const b=[...a];for(let i=b.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[b[i],b[j]]=[b[j],b[i]]}return b}
function loadState(){try{const r=localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);if(r)return JSON.parse(r)}catch(e){}return null}
function saveState(s){return safeStorageSet(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(s),'면접 학습 진도')}
function initBoxes(){
  const b={};
  QUESTIONS.forEach(q=>{
    b[q.id]={
      box:1, confidence:null, correct:0, wrong:0, lastStudied:null, firstStudied:null,
      needsRetry:false, recoveryStreak:0, lastRecoveryDate:null,
      // v4.51: SM-2 필드
      ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF, interval:0, reps:0, nextReview:null
    };
  });
  return b;
}

// v4.51: 레거시 데이터(v4.50 이하)에 SM-2 필드 주입
function migrateBoxes(loadedBoxes){
  if(!loadedBoxes) return loadedBoxes;
  Object.keys(loadedBoxes).forEach(id=>{
    const it = loadedBoxes[id];
    if(typeof it.ef !== 'number') it.ef = SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
    if(typeof it.interval !== 'number') it.interval = 0;
    if(typeof it.reps !== 'number') it.reps = 0;
    if(!('nextReview' in it)) it.nextReview = null;
    if(typeof it.needsRetry!=='boolean') it.needsRetry=(it.wrong||0)>0&&(it.box||1)<3;
    if(typeof it.recoveryStreak!=='number') it.recoveryStreak=0;
    if(!('lastRecoveryDate' in it)) it.lastRecoveryDate=null;
    // v4.84 이전 학습 기록은 첫 학습일을 알 수 없으므로 신규 목표에서 제외합니다.
    if(!('firstStudied' in it)) it.firstStudied=it.lastStudied?'legacy':null;
  });
  return loadedBoxes;
}

// v4.84: 처음 접한 날짜와 최근 학습 날짜를 분리해 하루 신규 목표를 정확히 계산합니다.
function stampStudied(item){
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  if(!item.firstStudied)item.firstStudied=now;
  item.lastStudied=now;
}

function updateWrongRecovery(item,correct,confidence,isMock){
  if(!correct){item.needsRetry=true;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;return;}
  if(confidence!=='sure'||!item.needsRetry)return;
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.lastRecoveryDate===today)return;
  item.lastRecoveryDate=today;
  item.recoveryStreak=(item.recoveryStreak||0)+1;
  if(item.recoveryStreak>=2){item.needsRetry=false;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;}
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>

        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;

          if(showFeedback){

          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]!==null)return;
  pastAnswers[pastIdx] = i;
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastNext(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]===null)return;
  if(pastIdx+1 >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  pastIdx++;
  renderPastCard();
}

function renderPastResult(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  currentMode='past-result';
  clearPastProgress();
  const total = pastQueue.length;
  const isNavi3Practice = pastReturnView === 'navi3-practice';
  const analysisGradeLabel = ((n3aData()&&n3aData().meta&&n3aData().meta.gradeLabel)||'항해사').replace(/\s*\(상선\)\s*/g,'');
  let correct = 0;
  const wrongs = [];
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) correct++;
    else wrongs.push({q, i, ans: pastAnswers[i]});
  });
  const pct = total ? Math.round(100*correct/total) : 0;
  // v4.46: 오답 자동 누적 + 정답은 오답노트에서 자동 제거 + 풀이 기록 저장 (단일 회차만)
  wrongs.forEach(({q,ans}) => addPastWrong(q, ans));
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) removePastWrong(pqid(q));
  });
  if(!pastAllYears && !pastReturnView && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession){
    recordPastSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, correct, total, pastMode);
  }
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now()-pastStartedAt)/1000);
  const mm = Math.floor(elapsed/60), ss = elapsed%60;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===pastSubjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px;margin-bottom:6px">${pastMode==='mock'?'모의시험 결과':'학습 결과'}</div>
      <h1 style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;margin:0;color:${gColor}">${isNavi3Practice?`${escapeHtml(analysisGradeLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(navi3PracticeLabel)}`:`${s?s.name:''} · 필기 기출`}</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:900;color:${gColor};line-height:1">${correct} / ${total}</div>
      <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-top:6px">정답률 ${pct}%</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);margin-top:4px">소요시간 ${mm}분 ${ss}초</div>
    </div>
    ${wrongs.length ? `
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">오답 ${wrongs.length}개</div>
        ${wrongs.slice(0,30).map(({q,i,ans})=>`
          <div style="padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #F1F5F9">
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:4px">${q._year}년 ${q['회차']}회 · ${q['과목']} · Q${q['번호']}</div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#DC2626">내 답: ${ans!==null?optMarkers[ans]+' '+escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][ans])):'(미응답)'}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#059669">정답: ${optMarkers[q['정답']]} ${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][q['정답']]))}</div>
            ${renderExplainBlock(q,{compact:true})}
          </div>
        `).join('')}
        ${wrongs.length>30?`<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted);margin-top:8px;text-align:center">... 외 ${wrongs.length-30}개 (상위 30개만 표시)</div>`:''}
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div class="card" style="text-align:center;background:#D1FAE5">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#065F46">모두 정답</div>
      </div>
    `}
    ${wrongs.length?'<button class="btn btn-green mb12" onclick="retryPastSessionWrongs()">이번 오답만 다시 풀기 ('+wrongs.length+'문제)</button>':''}
    <div class="flex gap8" style="margin-top:12px">
      ${isNavi3Practice?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">빈출 분석으로</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="restartNavi3FrequencyPractice()">같은 문제 다시 풀기</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">${pastAllYears?'다시 선택':'이 회차 다시'}</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="pickPrepType(pastSubjectId)">과목 홈</button>`}
    </div>
  `;
}

function escapeHtml(s){
  return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c){
    return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[c];
  });
}


const getImp=id=>IMP[id]||3;
const stars=n=>icon('star',12).repeat(n);
const BOX_INT={1:1,2:3,3:7};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const app=$("app");

// v4.51: SM-2 기반 개인화 간격 반복
// 각 문제마다 난이도(ef)/다음 복습 간격(interval)/연속 정답(reps)/다음 복습일(nextReview) 추적
// Leitner 박스는 UI/통계 지표로 그대로 유지, 스케줄은 SM-2 interval이 주도
const SM2_DEFAULT_EF = 2.5;
const SM2_MIN_EF = 1.3;

// SM-2 품질 점수 매핑 (이 앱의 3버튼 입력):
// ○ 맞음(sure)   -> q=5 (perfect)
// △ 애매(unsure) -> q=3 (correct with difficulty)
// ✕ 틀림         -> q=1 (incorrect)
function qualityFromInput(correct, confidence){
  if(!correct) return 1;
  if(confidence==='sure') return 5;
  return 3;
}

// 순수 SM-2 계산. item을 변이시키고 nextReview(ISO) 갱신.
function updateSM2(item, q){
  // EF 갱신 (q에 무관하게 항상 적용되는 게 원안)
  let ef = (typeof item.ef==='number') ? item.ef : SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  ef = ef + (0.1 - (5-q)*(0.08 + (5-q)*0.02));
  if(ef < SM2_MIN_EF) ef = SM2_MIN_EF;
  item.ef = Math.round(ef*1000)/1000;

  let reps = item.reps || 0;
  let interval;

  if(q < 3){
    // 실패 → 처음부터
    reps = 0;
    interval = 1;
  } else {
    if(reps === 0) interval = 1;
    else if(reps === 1) interval = 6;
    else interval = Math.round((item.interval||1) * item.ef);
    reps += 1;
  }

  item.reps = reps;
  item.interval = interval;
  const next = new Date();
  next.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  next.setDate(next.getDate() + interval);
  item.nextReview = next.toISOString();
}

// v4.83: 같은 날의 반복은 숙달 연습으로만 취급하고 간격복습 횟수는 하루 1회만 반영합니다.
// 같은 날 평가가 여러 개면 가장 낮은 품질을 사용해 과도한 복습 연장을 막습니다.
function studyDateKey(){return toLocalDateInput(new Date())}
function updateSpacedReview(item,q){
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.sm2Day!==today){
    item.sm2Day=today;
    item.sm2DayQuality=q;
    item.sm2DayBase={ef:item.ef,reps:item.reps,interval:item.interval,nextReview:item.nextReview};
    updateSM2(item,q);
    return;
  }
  const dailyQ=Math.min(typeof item.sm2DayQuality==='number'?item.sm2DayQuality:5,q);
  if(dailyQ===item.sm2DayQuality)return;
  const base=item.sm2DayBase||{};
  item.ef=typeof base.ef==='number'?base.ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  item.reps=typeof base.reps==='number'?base.reps:0;
  item.interval=typeof base.interval==='number'?base.interval:0;
  item.nextReview=base.nextReview||null;
  item.sm2DayQuality=dailyQ;
  updateSM2(item,dailyQ);
}

function shuffle(a){const b=[...a];for(let i=b.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[b[i],b[j]]=[b[j],b[i]]}return b}
function loadState(){try{const r=localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);if(r)return JSON.parse(r)}catch(e){}return null}
function saveState(s){return safeStorageSet(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(s),'면접 학습 진도')}
function initBoxes(){
  const b={};
  QUESTIONS.forEach(q=>{
    b[q.id]={
      box:1, confidence:null, correct:0, wrong:0, lastStudied:null, firstStudied:null,
      needsRetry:false, recoveryStreak:0, lastRecoveryDate:null,
      // v4.51: SM-2 필드
      ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF, interval:0, reps:0, nextReview:null
    };
  });
  return b;
}

// v4.51: 레거시 데이터(v4.50 이하)에 SM-2 필드 주입
function migrateBoxes(loadedBoxes){
  if(!loadedBoxes) return loadedBoxes;
  Object.keys(loadedBoxes).forEach(id=>{
    const it = loadedBoxes[id];
    if(typeof it.ef !== 'number') it.ef = SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
    if(typeof it.interval !== 'number') it.interval = 0;
    if(typeof it.reps !== 'number') it.reps = 0;
    if(!('nextReview' in it)) it.nextReview = null;
    if(typeof it.needsRetry!=='boolean') it.needsRetry=(it.wrong||0)>0&&(it.box||1)<3;
    if(typeof it.recoveryStreak!=='number') it.recoveryStreak=0;
    if(!('lastRecoveryDate' in it)) it.lastRecoveryDate=null;
    // v4.84 이전 학습 기록은 첫 학습일을 알 수 없으므로 신규 목표에서 제외합니다.
    if(!('firstStudied' in it)) it.firstStudied=it.lastStudied?'legacy':null;
  });
  return loadedBoxes;
}

// v4.84: 처음 접한 날짜와 최근 학습 날짜를 분리해 하루 신규 목표를 정확히 계산합니다.
function stampStudied(item){
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  if(!item.firstStudied)item.firstStudied=now;
  item.lastStudied=now;
}

function updateWrongRecovery(item,correct,confidence,isMock){
  if(!correct){item.needsRetry=true;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;return;}
  if(confidence!=='sure'||!item.needsRetry)return;
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.lastRecoveryDate===today)return;
  item.lastRecoveryDate=today;
  item.recoveryStreak=(item.recoveryStreak||0)+1;
  if(item.recoveryStreak>=2){item.needsRetry=false;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;}
}

// v4.51: nextReview 기반 복습 대상 판정 (fallback: Leitner)
function isDue(item){
  if(!item) return false;
  // SM-2 경로: nextReview 있으면 그것만 본다
  if(item.nextReview){
    return new Date(item.nextReview).getTime() <= Date.now();
  }
  // 레거시 fallback: lastStudied + Leitner BOX_INT
  if(!item.lastStudied) return false;
  const d = (Date.now()-new Date(item.lastStudied).getTime())/(1e3*60*60*24);
  return d >= (BOX_INT[item.box]||1);
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]!==null)return;
  pastAnswers[pastIdx] = i;
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastNext(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]===null)return;
  if(pastIdx+1 >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  pastIdx++;
  renderPastCard();
}

function renderPastResult(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  currentMode='past-result';
  clearPastProgress();
  const total = pastQueue.length;
  const isNavi3Practice = pastReturnView === 'navi3-practice';
  const analysisGradeLabel = ((n3aData()&&n3aData().meta&&n3aData().meta.gradeLabel)||'항해사').replace(/\s*\(상선\)\s*/g,'');
  let correct = 0;
  const wrongs = [];
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) correct++;
    else wrongs.push({q, i, ans: pastAnswers[i]});
  });
  const pct = total ? Math.round(100*correct/total) : 0;
  // v4.46: 오답 자동 누적 + 정답은 오답노트에서 자동 제거 + 풀이 기록 저장 (단일 회차만)
  wrongs.forEach(({q,ans}) => addPastWrong(q, ans));
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) removePastWrong(pqid(q));
  });
  if(!pastAllYears && !pastReturnView && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession){
    recordPastSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, correct, total, pastMode);
  }
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now()-pastStartedAt)/1000);
  const mm = Math.floor(elapsed/60), ss = elapsed%60;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===pastSubjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px;margin-bottom:6px">${pastMode==='mock'?'모의시험 결과':'학습 결과'}</div>
      <h1 style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;margin:0;color:${gColor}">${isNavi3Practice?`${escapeHtml(analysisGradeLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(navi3PracticeLabel)}`:`${s?s.name:''} · 필기 기출`}</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:900;color:${gColor};line-height:1">${correct} / ${total}</div>
      <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-top:6px">정답률 ${pct}%</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);margin-top:4px">소요시간 ${mm}분 ${ss}초</div>
    </div>
    ${wrongs.length ? `
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">오답 ${wrongs.length}개</div>
        ${wrongs.slice(0,30).map(({q,i,ans})=>`
          <div style="padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #F1F5F9">
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:4px">${q._year}년 ${q['회차']}회 · ${q['과목']} · Q${q['번호']}</div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#DC2626">내 답: ${ans!==null?optMarkers[ans]+' '+escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][ans])):'(미응답)'}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#059669">정답: ${optMarkers[q['정답']]} ${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][q['정답']]))}</div>
            ${renderExplainBlock(q,{compact:true})}
          </div>
        `).join('')}
        ${wrongs.length>30?`<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted);margin-top:8px;text-align:center">... 외 ${wrongs.length-30}개 (상위 30개만 표시)</div>`:''}
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div class="card" style="text-align:center;background:#D1FAE5">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#065F46">모두 정답</div>
      </div>
    `}
    ${wrongs.length?'<button class="btn btn-green mb12" onclick="retryPastSessionWrongs()">이번 오답만 다시 풀기 ('+wrongs.length+'문제)</button>':''}
    <div class="flex gap8" style="margin-top:12px">
      ${isNavi3Practice?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">빈출 분석으로</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="restartNavi3FrequencyPractice()">같은 문제 다시 풀기</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">${pastAllYears?'다시 선택':'이 회차 다시'}</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="pickPrepType(pastSubjectId)">과목 홈</button>`}
    </div>
  `;
}

function escapeHtml(s){
  return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c){
    return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[c];
  });
}


const getImp=id=>IMP[id]||3;
const stars=n=>icon('star',12).repeat(n);
const BOX_INT={1:1,2:3,3:7};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const app=$("app");

// v4.51: SM-2 기반 개인화 간격 반복
// 각 문제마다 난이도(ef)/다음 복습 간격(interval)/연속 정답(reps)/다음 복습일(nextReview) 추적
// Leitner 박스는 UI/통계 지표로 그대로 유지, 스케줄은 SM-2 interval이 주도
const SM2_DEFAULT_EF = 2.5;
const SM2_MIN_EF = 1.3;

// SM-2 품질 점수 매핑 (이 앱의 3버튼 입력):
// ○ 맞음(sure)   -> q=5 (perfect)
// △ 애매(unsure) -> q=3 (correct with difficulty)
// ✕ 틀림         -> q=1 (incorrect)
function qualityFromInput(correct, confidence){
  if(!correct) return 1;
  if(confidence==='sure') return 5;
  return 3;
}

// 순수 SM-2 계산. item을 변이시키고 nextReview(ISO) 갱신.
function updateSM2(item, q){
  // EF 갱신 (q에 무관하게 항상 적용되는 게 원안)
  let ef = (typeof item.ef==='number') ? item.ef : SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  ef = ef + (0.1 - (5-q)*(0.08 + (5-q)*0.02));
  if(ef < SM2_MIN_EF) ef = SM2_MIN_EF;
  item.ef = Math.round(ef*1000)/1000;

  let reps = item.reps || 0;
  let interval;

  if(q < 3){
    // 실패 → 처음부터
    reps = 0;
    interval = 1;
  } else {
    if(reps === 0) interval = 1;
    else if(reps === 1) interval = 6;
    else interval = Math.round((item.interval||1) * item.ef);
    reps += 1;
  }

  item.reps = reps;
  item.interval = interval;
  const next = new Date();
  next.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  next.setDate(next.getDate() + interval);
  item.nextReview = next.toISOString();
}

// v4.83: 같은 날의 반복은 숙달 연습으로만 취급하고 간격복습 횟수는 하루 1회만 반영합니다.
// 같은 날 평가가 여러 개면 가장 낮은 품질을 사용해 과도한 복습 연장을 막습니다.
function studyDateKey(){return toLocalDateInput(new Date())}
function updateSpacedReview(item,q){
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.sm2Day!==today){
    item.sm2Day=today;
    item.sm2DayQuality=q;
    item.sm2DayBase={ef:item.ef,reps:item.reps,interval:item.interval,nextReview:item.nextReview};
    updateSM2(item,q);
    return;
  }
  const dailyQ=Math.min(typeof item.sm2DayQuality==='number'?item.sm2DayQuality:5,q);
  if(dailyQ===item.sm2DayQuality)return;
  const base=item.sm2DayBase||{};
  item.ef=typeof base.ef==='number'?base.ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
  item.reps=typeof base.reps==='number'?base.reps:0;
  item.interval=typeof base.interval==='number'?base.interval:0;
  item.nextReview=base.nextReview||null;
  item.sm2DayQuality=dailyQ;
  updateSM2(item,dailyQ);
}

function shuffle(a){const b=[...a];for(let i=b.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[b[i],b[j]]=[b[j],b[i]]}return b}
function loadState(){try{const r=localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);if(r)return JSON.parse(r)}catch(e){}return null}
function saveState(s){return safeStorageSet(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(s),'면접 학습 진도')}
function initBoxes(){
  const b={};
  QUESTIONS.forEach(q=>{
    b[q.id]={
      box:1, confidence:null, correct:0, wrong:0, lastStudied:null, firstStudied:null,
      needsRetry:false, recoveryStreak:0, lastRecoveryDate:null,
      // v4.51: SM-2 필드
      ef:SM2_DEFAULT_EF, interval:0, reps:0, nextReview:null
    };
  });
  return b;
}

// v4.51: 레거시 데이터(v4.50 이하)에 SM-2 필드 주입
function migrateBoxes(loadedBoxes){
  if(!loadedBoxes) return loadedBoxes;
  Object.keys(loadedBoxes).forEach(id=>{
    const it = loadedBoxes[id];
    if(typeof it.ef !== 'number') it.ef = SM2_DEFAULT_EF;
    if(typeof it.interval !== 'number') it.interval = 0;
    if(typeof it.reps !== 'number') it.reps = 0;
    if(!('nextReview' in it)) it.nextReview = null;
    if(typeof it.needsRetry!=='boolean') it.needsRetry=(it.wrong||0)>0&&(it.box||1)<3;
    if(typeof it.recoveryStreak!=='number') it.recoveryStreak=0;
    if(!('lastRecoveryDate' in it)) it.lastRecoveryDate=null;
    // v4.84 이전 학습 기록은 첫 학습일을 알 수 없으므로 신규 목표에서 제외합니다.
    if(!('firstStudied' in it)) it.firstStudied=it.lastStudied?'legacy':null;
  });
  return loadedBoxes;
}

// v4.84: 처음 접한 날짜와 최근 학습 날짜를 분리해 하루 신규 목표를 정확히 계산합니다.
function stampStudied(item){
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  if(!item.firstStudied)item.firstStudied=now;
  item.lastStudied=now;
}

function updateWrongRecovery(item,correct,confidence,isMock){
  if(!correct){item.needsRetry=true;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;return;}
  if(confidence!=='sure'||!item.needsRetry)return;
  const today=studyDateKey();
  if(item.lastRecoveryDate===today)return;
  item.lastRecoveryDate=today;
  item.recoveryStreak=(item.recoveryStreak||0)+1;
  if(item.recoveryStreak>=2){item.needsRetry=false;item.recoveryStreak=0;item.lastRecoveryDate=null;}
}

// v4.51: nextReview 기반 복습 대상 판정 (fallback: Leitner)
function isDue(item){
  if(!item) return false;
  // SM-2 경로: nextReview 있으면 그것만 본다
  if(item.nextReview){
    return new Date(item.nextReview).getTime() <= Date.now();
  }
  // 레거시 fallback: lastStudied + Leitner BOX_INT
  if(!item.lastStudied) return false;
  const d = (Date.now()-new Date(item.lastStudied).getTime())/(1e3*60*60*24);
  return d >= (BOX_INT[item.box]||1);
}
function impTag(id){const i=getImp(id);return i>=5?'tag-imp5':i>=4?'tag-imp4':'tag-imp-low'}
function boxTag(b){return b===1?'tag-red':b===2?'tag-amber':'tag-green'}

let boxes={};
let queue=[], currentIdx=0, hintLevel=0, showAnswer=false, filter="all", catFilter="all";
let answerCollapsed=false; // v4.62: 모범답안 접기/펼치기 상태
let sessionResults=[], mockPhase=0, currentMode="home";
let homeToolsOpen=false;

// ── v4.82: 2급 항해사 표준 도판 훈련 ──
const NAVI2_VISUAL_DRILLS = [
