async function ensurePastDataForSubject(subjectId){
  const files=getPastDataFilesForSubject(subjectId);
  if(!files.length) return [];
  const results=await loadScriptsWithLimit(files,8);
  const failedFiles=files.filter((file,index)=>{
    const registryId=file.slice(5,-3);
    return !results[index] || !window.MD_PAST || !window.MD_PAST[registryId];
  });
  if(failedFiles.length){
    const error=new Error(`기출 파일 ${failedFiles.length}개 로드 실패`);
    error.failedFiles=failedFiles;
    throw error;
  }
  pastDataSubjectsLoaded.add(subjectId);
  return getPastExamsForSubject(subjectId);
}
async function ensurePastExplainForYears(years){
  const targets=[...new Set((years||[]).map(Number))]
    .filter(year=>PAST_EXPLAIN_YEARS.has(year))
    .map(year=>`past-explain-${year}.js`);
  if(!targets.length) return {failedFiles:[]};
  const results=await loadScriptsWithLimit(targets,2);
  const failedFiles=targets.filter((file,index)=>!results[index]);
  if(failedFiles.length && typeof showToast==='function'){
    showToast(`해설 파일 ${failedFiles.length}개를 불러오지 못했습니다. 문제 풀이는 계속할 수 있습니다.`);
  }
  return {failedFiles};
}
// v4.34: 변종(short) → base(상선/어선 합친 공통 코드) 매핑
//   navi3e→navi3, navi5de→navi5, engi3→engi3, small→small
// v4.45: engi5d→engi5 (5급 기관사 국내한정 변종)
function baseFromShort(short){
  if(!short) return short;
  // navi 계열: 'e'/'de'/'d' 접미사 제거 (어선·국내한정 접미사)
  if(/^navi\d/.test(short)) return short.replace(/(de|e|d)$/,'');
  // engi 계열: 'd' 접미사 제거 (국내한정 접미사, 현재 5급만)
  if(/^engi\d/.test(short)) return short.replace(/d$/,'');
  return short;
}
// v4.34: base gradeShort → "3급 항해사" 같이 변종 괄호를 제거한 상위 라벨
const BASE_GRADE_LABEL = {
  'navi1':'1급 항해사','navi2':'2급 항해사','navi3':'3급 항해사','navi4':'4급 항해사',
  'navi5':'5급 항해사','navi6':'6급 항해사',
  'engi1':'1급 기관사','engi2':'2급 기관사','engi3':'3급 기관사','engi4':'4급 기관사',
  'engi5':'5급 기관사','engi6':'6급 기관사',
  'small':'소형선박조종사'
};
// v4.34: short(변종) → 상선/어선/국내한정 구분 라벨 ('' 이면 구분 불필요)
// v4.45: engi5/engi5d → '일반'/'국내한정' 구분 추가
function variantLabel(short){
  if(/^navi\d/.test(short)){
    const m = short.match(/^navi\d(de|e|d)?$/);
    if(!m) return '';
    const suf = m[1] || '';
    if(suf === '') return '상선';
    if(suf === 'd') return '국내한정(상선)';
    if(suf === 'e') return '어선';
    if(suf === 'de') return '국내한정(어선)';
  }
  if(/^engi\d/.test(short)){
    if(short === 'engi5')  return '일반';
    if(short === 'engi5d') return '국내한정';
  }
  return '';
}
// v4.34: variant='all'|'상선'|'어선' 필터 통과 여부
// v4.45: engi5 계열용 '일반'/'국내한정' variant 추가
function variantMatches(short, variant){
  if(!variant || variant === 'all') return true;
  const lab = variantLabel(short);
  if(variant === '상선') return lab === '상선' || lab === '국내한정(상선)';
  if(variant === '어선') return lab === '어선' || lab === '국내한정(어선)';
  if(variant === '일반') return lab === '일반';
  if(variant === '국내한정') return lab === '국내한정';
  return true;
}
// v4.45: 현재 exams 풀에서 노출할 variant 필터 (title + 선택지)를 결정.
//   navi: '상선'/'어선' (국내한정은 각각에 합류)
//   engi5: '일반'/'국내한정'
//   필터가 불필요(변종 1종 이하)하면 choices=[]
function getVariantChoices(exams){
  const labs = [...new Set(exams.map(p => variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort)).filter(Boolean))];
  const hasSS = labs.some(l => l.indexOf('상선')>=0);
  const hasYU = labs.some(l => l.indexOf('어선')>=0);
  if(hasSS && hasYU){
    return {title:'상선 / 어선', choices:['상선','어선']};
  }
  const hasNormal = labs.includes('일반');
  const hasRestrict = labs.includes('국내한정');
  if(hasNormal && hasRestrict){
    return {title:'일반 / 국내한정', choices:['일반','국내한정']};
  }
  return {title:'', choices:[]};
}
function getPastExamsForSubject(subjectId){
  if(!window.MD_PAST) return [];
  const shorts = PAST_GRADE_MAP[subjectId] || [subjectId];
  return Object.values(window.MD_PAST).filter(p => p.meta && shorts.includes(p.meta.gradeShort));
}
// v4.34: base(예: 'navi3')에 속한 모든 변종(navi3+navi3e) 시험 반환
function getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant){
  if(!window.MD_PAST) return [];
  return Object.values(window.MD_PAST).filter(p => {
    if(!p.meta) return false;
    if(baseFromShort(p.meta.gradeShort) !== baseShort) return false;
    return variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, variant);
  });
}
function getPastExam(short, year, session){
  if(!window.MD_PAST) return null;
  const id = `${year}-${short}-${session}`;
  return window.MD_PAST[id] || null;
}

// ── v5.00: 1~5급 항해사(상선) 공통 빈출 분석 ──
const NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES=['navi1','navi2','navi3','navi4','navi5'];
const NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT=50;
const naviAnalysisStates={};
let naviAnalysisSubjectId='navi3';
let navi3AnalysisState=naviAnalysisStates.navi3={view:'repeats',subject:'all',range:'all',subjects:null,years:null,minCount:2,sort:'score',query:'',limit:NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT,_filterLoaded:false};
let navi3AnalysisDetail=null;
const N3A_FILTER_STORAGE_PREFIX='md_navi_frequency_filters_v1_';
function n3aNormalizeQuestion(value){return String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s\p{P}\p{S}]+/gu,'')}
function n3aFilterStorageKey(){return `${N3A_FILTER_STORAGE_PREFIX}${naviAnalysisSubjectId}`}
function n3aEnsureFilterState(data){
  const state=navi3AnalysisState;
  const subjects=(data&&data.subjects||[]).map(String);
  const years=(data&&data.meta&&data.meta.years||[]).map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(!state._filterLoaded){
    const saved=safeStorageGetJSON(n3aFilterStorageKey(),{},'빈출 필터');
    state.subjects=Array.isArray(saved.subjects)?saved.subjects.map(String):subjects.slice();
    state.years=Array.isArray(saved.years)?saved.years.map(Number):years.slice();
    state.minCount=[1,2,3,4].includes(Number(saved.minCount))?Number(saved.minCount):2;
    state._filterLoaded=true;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(state.subjects))state.subjects=subjects.slice();
  if(!Array.isArray(state.years))state.years=years.slice();
  state.subjects=state.subjects.filter(subject=>subjects.includes(subject));
  state.years=state.years.map(Number).filter(year=>years.includes(year));
  if(![1,2,3,4].includes(Number(state.minCount)))state.minCount=2;
  return state;
}
function n3aSaveFilterState(){
  const state=navi3AnalysisState;
  safeStorageSet(n3aFilterStorageKey(),JSON.stringify({subjects:state.subjects||[],years:state.years||[],minCount:Number(state.minCount)||2}),'빈출 필터');
}
function n3aSubjectSelected(subject){return Array.isArray(navi3AnalysisState.subjects)&&navi3AnalysisState.subjects.includes(subject)}
function n3aToggleSubject(subject,checked){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return;
  n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  const set=new Set(navi3AnalysisState.subjects||[]);
  checked?set.add(subject):set.delete(subject);
  navi3AnalysisState.subjects=(data.subjects||[]).filter(item=>set.has(item));
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;n3aSaveFilterState();renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aPresetSubjects(mode){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return;n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  navi3AnalysisState.subjects=mode==='none'?[]:[...(data.subjects||[])];
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;n3aSaveFilterState();renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aToggleYear(year,checked){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return;n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  const set=new Set((navi3AnalysisState.years||[]).map(Number));
  checked?set.add(Number(year)):set.delete(Number(year));
  navi3AnalysisState.years=(data.meta.years||[]).map(Number).filter(item=>set.has(item));
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;n3aSaveFilterState();renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aPresetYears(mode,skipRender){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return;n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  const all=(data.meta.years||[]).map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const partial=new Set((data.meta.partialYears||[]).map(Number));
  const complete=((data.meta.completeYears||[]).length?(data.meta.completeYears||[]).map(Number):all.filter(year=>!partial.has(year))).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(mode==='recent5')navi3AnalysisState.years=complete.slice(-5);
  else if(mode==='excludeLatest'){
    const latest=Math.max(...all);
    navi3AnalysisState.years=all.filter(year=>year!==latest);
  }else navi3AnalysisState.years=all;
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;n3aSaveFilterState();if(!skipRender)renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aSetMinCount(value,skipRender){
  const n=Number(value);navi3AnalysisState.minCount=[1,2,3,4].includes(n)?n:2;
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;n3aSaveFilterState();if(!skipRender)renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aQuickRecentFive(){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return;n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  const all=(data.meta.years||[]).map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const partial=new Set((data.meta.partialYears||[]).map(Number));
  const complete=((data.meta.completeYears||[]).length?(data.meta.completeYears||[]).map(Number):all.filter(year=>!partial.has(year))).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  navi3AnalysisState.years=complete.slice(-5);
  navi3AnalysisState.minCount=2;
  navi3AnalysisState.view='repeats';
  navi3AnalysisState.sort='count';
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;
  n3aSaveFilterState();renderNavi3Frequency();
}

function n3aActivateGrade(subjectId){
  if(!NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(subjectId))subjectId='navi3';
  if(naviAnalysisSubjectId!==subjectId)navi3AnalysisDetail=null;
  naviAnalysisSubjectId=subjectId;
  if(!naviAnalysisStates[subjectId])naviAnalysisStates[subjectId]={view:'repeats',subject:'all',range:'all',subjects:null,years:null,minCount:2,sort:'score',query:'',limit:NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT,_filterLoaded:false};
  navi3AnalysisState=naviAnalysisStates[subjectId];
}
function n3aData(subjectId){
  const gradeId=subjectId||naviAnalysisSubjectId;
  return window.MD_NAVI_FREQUENCY&&window.MD_NAVI_FREQUENCY[gradeId]||null;
}
const n3aOriginIndexes={};
function n3aOriginKey(year,session,subject,number){return `${year}|${session}|${subject}|${number}`}
function n3aQuestionOrigin(q){
  if(!q||Number(q._year)<2026)return null;
  const gradeId=baseFromShort(q._short||'');
  if(!NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(gradeId))return null;
  const data=n3aData(gradeId);
  if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.classifications))return null;
  if(!n3aOriginIndexes[gradeId]){
    n3aOriginIndexes[gradeId]=new Map(data.classifications.map(entry=>[
      n3aOriginKey(entry.year,entry.session,entry.subject,entry.number),entry
    ]));
  }
  return n3aOriginIndexes[gradeId].get(n3aOriginKey(q._year,q._session||q['회차'],q['과목'],q['번호']))||null;
}
function n3aOriginTagHtml(q){
  const origin=n3aQuestionOrigin(q);if(!origin)return '';
  const styles={
    new:{label:'신출',bg:'#F3E8FF',color:'#7E22CE'},
    past:{label:'기출',bg:'#DBEAFE',color:'#1D4ED8'},
    frequent:{label:'빈출',bg:'#FEE2E2',color:'#B91C1C'}
  };
  const style=styles[origin.status]||styles.new;
  const history=origin.previousCount
    ? `2020~2025년 동일 문항 ${origin.previousCount}회 · ${origin.previousYears.join('·')}년`
    : '2020~2025년 동일 문항 없음';
  const sameYear=origin.sameYearEarlierCount?` · ${origin.year}년 앞 회차 ${origin.sameYearEarlierCount}회`:'';
  const count=origin.status==='frequent'?` · 과거 ${origin.previousCount}회`:origin.status==='past'?' · 과거 1회':'';
  return `<span class="tag" style="background:${style.bg};color:${style.color};margin:0" title="${escapeHtml(history+sameYear)}">${style.label}${count}</span>`;
}
async function ensureNavi3FrequencyData(subjectId){
  const gradeId=subjectId||naviAnalysisSubjectId;
  const existing=n3aData(gradeId);if(existing)return existing;
  const loaded=await loadOptionalScript(`past-analysis-${gradeId}.js`);
  const data=n3aData(gradeId);
  if(!loaded||!data)throw new Error(`${gradeId.replace('navi','')}급 항해사 분석 데이터를 불러오지 못했습니다.`);
  return data;
}
function n3aRangeYears(data,range){
  const all=(data.meta.years||[]).map(Number);
  n3aEnsureFilterState(data);
  if(Array.isArray(navi3AnalysisState.years))return new Set(navi3AnalysisState.years.map(Number).filter(year=>all.includes(year)));
  if(range==='complete')return new Set((data.meta.completeYears||[]).map(Number));
  if(range==='recent')return new Set(all.filter(year=>year>=2023));
  return new Set(all);
}
function n3aRangeLabel(data,range){
  const years=[...n3aRangeYears(data,range)].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(!years.length)return '선택 연도 없음';
  const contiguous=years.every((year,index)=>index===0||year===years[index-1]+1);
  const label=contiguous&&years.length>1?`${years[0]}~${years[years.length-1]}년`:`${years.join('·')}년`;
  const partial=(data.meta.partialYears||[]).map(Number).filter(year=>years.includes(year));
  return `${label}${partial.length?` · ${partial.join('·')} 부분 포함`:''}`;
}
function n3aYearSpan(years){
  const sorted=(years||[]).map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  return sorted.length?`${sorted[0]}~${sorted[sorted.length-1]}`:'자료 없음';
}
function n3aFilteredHits(hits,yearSet){return (hits||[]).filter(hit=>yearSet.has(Number(hit.year)))}
function n3aSessionKey(hit){return `${hit.year}-${hit.session}`}
function n3aUniqueYears(hits){return [...new Set(hits.map(hit=>Number(hit.year)))].sort((a,b)=>a-b)}
function n3aTier(score){
  if(score>=80)return {label:'최우선',cls:'n3a-score-top'};
  if(score>=65)return {label:'중요',cls:'n3a-score-high'};
  if(score>=50)return {label:'주목',cls:'n3a-score-watch'};
  return {label:'보통',cls:'n3a-score-normal'};
}
function n3aRecencyScore(latest,maxYear){return Math.max(4,25-Math.max(0,maxYear-latest)*4)}
function n3aRepeatScore(hits,yearSet){
  const years=n3aUniqueYears(hits),maxYear=Math.max(...yearSet);
  return Math.min(100,Math.round(Math.min(60,hits.length/7*60)+n3aRecencyScore(years[years.length-1],maxYear)+Math.min(15,years.length/6*15)));
}
function n3aTopicScore(hits,yearSet,totalSessions){
  const years=n3aUniqueYears(hits),latest=years[years.length-1];
  const sessions=new Set(hits.map(n3aSessionKey)).size;
  const coverage=totalSessions?Math.min(1,sessions/totalSessions):0;
  const density=totalSessions?Math.min(1,hits.length/(totalSessions*4)):0;
  return Math.min(100,Math.round(coverage*55+density*20+n3aRecencyScore(latest,Math.max(...yearSet))));
}
function n3aAllSessionKeys(data,yearSet){
  const keys=new Set();
  (data.groups||[]).forEach(group=>n3aFilteredHits(group.hits,yearSet).forEach(hit=>keys.add(n3aSessionKey(hit))));
  return keys;
}
function n3aRangeStats(data,yearSet){
  let questions=0,repeated=0;
  const minCount=Number(navi3AnalysisState.minCount)||2;
  (data.groups||[]).forEach(group=>{
    if(!n3aSubjectSelected(group.subject))return;
    const count=n3aFilteredHits(group.hits,yearSet).length;
    questions+=count;
    if(count>=minCount)repeated+=1;
  });
  return {questions,repeated,sessions:n3aAllSessionKeys(data,yearSet).size};
}
function n3aBuildEntries(data){
  const state=navi3AnalysisState;
  const yearSet=n3aRangeYears(data,state.range);
  const query=String(state.query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const totalSessions=n3aAllSessionKeys(data,yearSet).size;
  let entries=[];
  if(state.view==='topics'){
    entries=(data.topics||[]).map(topic=>{
      const hits=n3aFilteredHits(topic.hits,yearSet);
      const years=n3aUniqueYears(hits);
      const sessions=new Set(hits.map(n3aSessionKey));
      return {...topic,hits,years,latest:years[years.length-1]||0,sessionCount:sessions.size,occurrences:hits.length,distinctQuestions:new Set(hits.map(hit=>hit.groupId)).size,score:hits.length?n3aTopicScore(hits,yearSet,totalSessions):0};
    }).filter(entry=>entry.hits.length&&
      n3aSubjectSelected(entry.subject)&&
      (!query||`${entry.label} ${entry.subject}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)));
  }else{
    entries=(data.groups||[]).map(group=>{
      const hits=n3aFilteredHits(group.hits,yearSet);
      const years=n3aUniqueYears(hits);
      return {...group,hits,years,count:hits.length,latest:years[years.length-1]||0,score:hits.length?n3aRepeatScore(hits,yearSet):0};
    }).filter(entry=>entry.count>=(Number(state.minCount)||2)&&
      n3aSubjectSelected(entry.subject)&&
      (!query||`${entry.question} ${entry.subject}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)));
  }
  entries.sort((a,b)=>{
    if(state.sort==='count')return (b.occurrences||b.count)-(a.occurrences||a.count)||b.score-a.score;
    if(state.sort==='recent')return b.latest-a.latest||b.score-a.score;
    return b.score-a.score||(b.occurrences||b.count)-(a.occurrences||a.count);
  });
  return entries;
}
function n3aSetState(key,value){
  navi3AnalysisState[key]=value;
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;
  renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aApplySearch(){
  const input=document.getElementById('n3a-search');
  navi3AnalysisState.query=input?input.value.trim():'';
  navi3AnalysisState.limit=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;
  renderNavi3Frequency();
}
function n3aMore(){navi3AnalysisState.limit+=NAVI3_ANALYSIS_LIMIT;renderNavi3Frequency()}
function n3aScoreHtml(score){const tier=n3aTier(score);return `<span class="n3a-score ${tier.cls}">${tier.label} · ${score}</span>`}

// v4.98: 빈출 통계를 실제 문제 풀이 세션으로 연결합니다.
let navi3PracticeReturn=null;
let navi3PracticeLabel='빈출 문제';
function n3aPracticeGroups(data,source,id){
  const yearSet=n3aRangeYears(data,navi3AnalysisState.range);
  const groupsById=new Map((data.groups||[]).map(group=>[group.id,group]));
  const toEntry=group=>{
    if(!group)return null;
    const hits=n3aFilteredHits(group.hits,yearSet);
    const years=n3aUniqueYears(hits);
    return hits.length?{...group,hits,years,count:hits.length,latest:years[years.length-1]||0,score:n3aRepeatScore(hits,yearSet)}:null;
  };
  let entries=[];
  if(source==='group'){
    entries=[toEntry(groupsById.get(id))].filter(Boolean);
  }else if(source==='topic'){
    const topic=(data.topics||[]).find(item=>item.id===id);
    const groupIds=new Set(n3aFilteredHits(topic&&topic.hits,yearSet).map(hit=>hit.groupId));
    entries=[...groupIds].map(groupId=>toEntry(groupsById.get(groupId))).filter(Boolean);
  }else if(source==='filtered'){
    const query=String(navi3AnalysisState.query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    entries=(data.groups||[]).map(toEntry).filter(entry=>entry&&
      entry.count>=(Number(navi3AnalysisState.minCount)||2)&&
      n3aSubjectSelected(entry.subject)&&
      (!query||`${entry.question} ${entry.subject}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)));
  }else if(navi3AnalysisState.view==='repeats'){
    entries=n3aBuildEntries(data);
  }else{
    const topicEntries=n3aBuildEntries(data);
    const groupIds=new Set(topicEntries.flatMap(topic=>topic.hits.map(hit=>hit.groupId)));
    entries=[...groupIds].map(groupId=>toEntry(groupsById.get(groupId))).filter(Boolean);
  }
  entries.sort((a,b)=>{
    if(navi3AnalysisState.sort==='count')return b.count-a.count||b.score-a.score||b.latest-a.latest;
    if(navi3AnalysisState.sort==='recent')return b.latest-a.latest||b.score-a.score||b.count-a.count;
    return b.score-a.score||b.count-a.count||b.latest-a.latest;
  });
  return entries;
}
function n3aFindQuestion(group,hit){
  const exam=getPastExam(naviAnalysisSubjectId,hit.year,hit.session);
  return exam&&exam.questions.find(item=>item['과목']===group.subject&&Number(item['번호'])===Number(hit.number)&&n3aNormalizeQuestion(item['문제'])===group.normalized);
}
async function startNavi3FrequencyPractice(source,id,limit,mode,returnKind,returnId){
  const safeMode=mode==='mock'?'mock':'study';
  const useAll=limit==='all';
  const maxQuestions=useAll?Infinity:Math.max(1,Number(limit)||1);
  const returnTarget={kind:returnKind||'list',id:returnId||null};
  renderPastLoading(safeMode==='mock'?'빈출 모의시험을 준비하는 중입니다':'빈출 학습 문제를 준비하는 중입니다');
  try{
    const data=await ensureNavi3FrequencyData();
    const allCandidates=n3aPracticeGroups(data,source||'current',id);
    const candidates=useAll?allCandidates:allCandidates.slice(0,maxQuestions);
    if(!candidates.length)throw new Error('현재 조건에서 풀 수 있는 문제가 없습니다.');
    const gradeId=naviAnalysisSubjectId;
    if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(gradeId))await ensurePastDataForSubject(gradeId);
    const selected=candidates;
    const pool=[];
    for(const group of selected){
      const hit=[...group.hits].sort((a,b)=>b.year-a.year||b.session-a.session||b.number-a.number)[0];
      const question=hit&&n3aFindQuestion(group,hit);
      if(question)pool.push({...question,_year:hit.year,_session:hit.session,'회차':hit.session,_short:gradeId,_n3aGroupId:group.id});
    }
    if(!pool.length)throw new Error('기출 원문과 연결되는 문제를 찾지 못했습니다.');
    await ensurePastExplainForYears(pool.map(question=>question._year));
    pastSubjectId=gradeId;pastBaseShort=gradeId;pastYear=null;pastSession=null;pastVariant='상선';pastAllYears=false;
    pastQueue=pool;pastIdx=0;pastAnswers=new Array(pool.length).fill(null);pastMode=safeMode;pastStartedAt=Date.now();
    pastReturnView='navi3-practice';navi3PracticeReturn=returnTarget;
    navi3PracticeLabel=source==='topic'?'빈출 주제':source==='group'?'반복 문항':'현재 조건 빈출';
    currentMode='past';renderPastCard();
  }catch(error){
    const back=returnTarget.kind==='topic'&&returnTarget.id?`renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic','${returnTarget.id}')`:'renderNavi3Frequency()';
    app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px;text-align:center;border-left:4px solid #DC2626"><div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#991B1B">빈출 문제를 준비하지 못했습니다</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin:7px 0 15px">${escapeHtml(error.message||String(error))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="${back}">분석으로 돌아가기</button></div>`;
  }
}
function restartNavi3FrequencyPractice(){
  if(!pastQueue.length){renderNavi3Frequency();return}
  pastIdx=0;pastAnswers=new Array(pastQueue.length).fill(null);pastStartedAt=Date.now();currentMode='past';renderPastCard();
}

async function renderNavi3Frequency(subjectId){
  if(subjectId)n3aActivateGrade(subjectId);
  stopTTS();stopMockTimer();currentMode='analysis';
  let data=n3aData();
  if(!data){
    renderPastLoading(`${naviAnalysisSubjectId.replace('navi','')}급 항해사 빈출 통계를 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{data=await ensureNavi3FrequencyData()}catch(error){
      ap

--- 2 @ 22218766 ---
v>`:''}
    </div>
    ` : `
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:40px 20px;background:#F8FAFC">
      <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:4px">북마크가 없습니다</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted)">풀이 중 북마크 버튼으로 저장해 두세요 (단축키 B)</div>
    </div>`}
  `;
}

// 오답/북마크 전용 세션 시작
async function startPastSpecialSession(kind){
  pastReturnView=null;
  const m = kind==='wrong' ? loadPastWrongs() : loadPastMarks();
  const arr = Object.values(m).map(e => ({...e.q, _source: kind}));
  if(!arr.length){ showToast(kind==='wrong'?'오답이 없습니다.':'북마크된 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('저장한 문제의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(arr.map(q=>q._year));
  pastQueue = shuffle(arr);
  pastSubjectId=baseFromShort(pastQueue[0]._short||pastQueue[0]._gradeShort);pastBaseShort=pastSubjectId;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pastQueue.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = 'study';
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;           // 결과 후 goBackFromPastSession이 홈으로 돌아가도록
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// Step 1: 해당 subject에 속한 세부 급(5급/6급 등) 선택
// v4.34: 상선/어선 변종을 base로 통합. base 버킷이 1개면 자동으로 년도 선택으로 점프
async function renderPastHome(subjectId){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===subjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  if(!s || !getPastDataFilesForSubject(subjectId).length){ renderSubjectSelect(); return; }
  let pastList = getPastExamsForSubject(subjectId);
  if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(subjectId)){
    renderPastLoading(`${s.name} 기출 데이터를 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{
      pastList = await ensurePastDataForSubject(subjectId);
    }catch(error){
      console.error('[v4.81] 기출 데이터 로드 실패:',error);
      renderPastLoadError(subjectId,error.failedFiles||[]);
      return;
    }
  }
  if(!s || !pastList.length){ renderSubjectSelect(); return; }
  // v4.34: base 기준으로 세부 급 묶기 (navi3 + navi3e → 'navi3' / engi5,engi6 → 별도)
  const bucket = new Map(); // baseShort -> {label, count}
  pastList.forEach(p => {
    const sh = p.meta.gradeShort || 'x';
    const base = baseFromShort(sh);
    const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[base] || (p.meta.gradeLabel || base).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
    const b = bucket.get(base) || {label, count: 0};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    bucket.set(base, b);
  });
  const bases = [...bucket.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));

  // v4.34 Change 3: base 버킷이 1개뿐이면 자동으로 년도 선택으로 점프 (중복 네비 제거)
  if(bases.length === 1){
    const [onlyBase] = bases[0];
    renderPastYearPick(subjectId, onlyBase);
    return;
  }

  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="pickPrepType('${subjectId}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 학습유형으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${s.name} <span style="font-size:15px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 필기 기출</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">세부 급을 선택하세요</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${bases.map(([base,b])=>`
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${base}')">
            ${b.label} <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:center;padding:16px 0">
      <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--textMuted);letter-spacing:1px">Maritime Drill v${APP_VERSION} · 필기 기출</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// Step 2: 년도 선택
// v4.34: 인자명 short → baseShort (상선/어선 통합). 내부는 변종 exams 모두 포함.
// v4.38: 과목별 모아보기(Subject Aggregate) 진입점 제거 — runner에서 동일 기능 제공.
// v4.44: 최상단에 "전 년도 랜덤" 카드 추가 — 과목/변종 필터로 전 년도 통합 학습·모의 가능.
function renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===subjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all');
  if(!exams.length){ renderPastHome(subjectId); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (exams[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // v4.45: 현재 exam 풀에 실제 존재하는 variant로 동적 필터 구성 (navi: 상선/어선, engi5: 일반/국내한정)
  const vc = getVariantChoices(exams);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastYearPickVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  // 년도별 집계 (변종 합산)
  const years = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => {
    const y = p.meta.year;
    const b = years.get(y) || {count:0, sessions:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    b.sessions.add(p.meta.year + '-' + p.meta.gradeShort + '-' + p.meta.session);
    years.set(y, b);
  });
  const yearList = [...years.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[0]-a[0]);
  const totalQ = exams.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0);
  // v4.44: 전 년도 통합 풀 (현재 변종 필터 적용) — 과목별 수 집계
  const poolExams = exams.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  const subjCountAll = new Map();
  poolExams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCountAll.set(k, (subjCountAll.get(k)||0)+1);
  }));
  const subjectsAll = [...subjCountAll.entries()];
  const allYearsMockCount = subjectsAll.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  // Back 버튼: 버킷이 1개였던 과목(small/engi1/engi2/navi1~4)은 renderPastHome이 자동 점프하므로
  // 학습유형으로 바로 돌아가게 한다.
  const allBases = new Set(getPastExamsForSubject(subjectId).map(p => baseFromShort(p.meta.gradeShort)));
  const backHandler = allBases.size <= 1 ? `pickPrepType('${subjectId}')` : `renderPastHome('${subjectId}')`;
  const backLabel = allBases.size <= 1 ? '학습유형으로' : '급 선택으로';
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastYearPickVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="${backHandler}">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} ${backLabel}</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 년도 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${yearList.length}개 년도 · 총 ${totalQ}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">전 년도 랜덤</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">${yearList[yearList.length-1][0]}~${yearList[0][0]}년 ${poolExams.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제 통합 풀 · 과목당 랜덤 추출</div>
      ${showVariantFilter ? `
      <div style="margin-bottom:12px">
        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:6px">${vc.title}</div>
        <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
          ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>` : ''}
      <div id="past-allyears-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjectsAll.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-allyears-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastAllYearsSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastAllYearsSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastAllYearsSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastAllYearsSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">년도 선택 랜덤</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">원하는 년도만 골라서 랜덤 출제</div>
      <div id="past-yearcheck-list" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${yearList.map(([y,b])=>{
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-year-check" value="${y}" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${y}년</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${b.count}문제</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('study')">선택한 년도 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('mock')">선택한 년도 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">년도별 회차 선택</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${yearList.map(([y,b])=>`
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${y})">
            ${y}년 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${b.sessions.size}회차 · ${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경
function setPastYearPickVariant(v){
  pastYearPickVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort) renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면 과목 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastAllYearsSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
function togglePastYearChecks(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
async function startPastYearPickSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const years = [...document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check:checked')].map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
  if(!years.length){ showToast('년도를 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, pastVariant||'all')
    .filter(p => years.includes(p.meta.year));
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('해당 년도의 데이터가 없습니다.'); return; }
  const pool = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => pool.push({
    ...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  // 과목별 분리 후 모의시험 or 학습
  var finalPool;
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    var bySubj2 = new Map();
    pool.forEach(function(q){
      var k = q['과목']||'기타';
      if(!bySubj2.has(k)) bySubj2.set(k,[]);
      bySubj2.get(k).push(q);
    });
    finalPool = [];
    bySubj2.forEach(function(qs){
      finalPool.push.apply(finalPool, shuffle(qs.slice()).slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    finalPool = shuffle(finalPool);
  } else {
    finalPool = shuffle(pool.slice());
  }
  renderPastLoading('선택한 년도의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(years);
  pastAllYears=true;pastYear=null;pastSession=null;pastReturnView=null;
  pastQueue = finalPool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(finalPool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션 시작 — 과목 체크박스 + 변종 필터 기준
async function startPastAllYearsSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  const filterSet = new Set(picked);
  // 과목별로 모으기 (전 년도 전 회차)
  const bySubj = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push({...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort});
  }));
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('전체 년도 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(exams.map(p=>p.meta.year));
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;         // v4.44: 그만/결과 후 년도 선택으로 복귀하도록 플래그
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// v4.38: renderPastSubjectAggregate + setPastVariant + toggleAggSubjects + startPastAggregateSession 제거.
//        동일 기능(과목체크/상선어선 필터/학습·모의)을 renderPastRunner가 회차 단위로 완전 대체.

// Step 3: 회차 선택
// v4.35: 상선/어선 변종을 하나의 회차 버튼으로 통합(제N회 정기시험 × 1개).
//        상선/어선 구분은 다음 화면(runner)의 필터에서 처리.
function renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all')
    .filter(p=>p.meta.year===year);
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // 회차별 집계 (변종 합산: 제1회 상선+제1회 어선 → 한 행)
  const bySession = new Map(); // session -> {count, variants:Set}
  examsAll.forEach(p => {
    const s = p.meta.session;
    const b = bySession.get(s) || {count:0, variants:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    const vl = variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort);
    if(vl) b.variants.add(vl);
    bySession.set(s, b);
  });
  const sessionList = [...bySession.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 년도 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 ${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 회차 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${sessionList.length}회차 · 총 ${examsAll.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${sessionList.map(([sess,b])=>{
          // v4.46: 풀이 기록 뱃지 (최고 정답률 · 시도 횟수)
          const hist = getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, sess);
          const bestPct = hist ? Math.round(hist.best*100) : null;
          const badge = hist
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${bestPct>=80?'#D1FAE5':bestPct>=60?'#FEF3C7':'#FEE2E2'};color:${bestPct>=80?'#065F46':bestPct>=60?'#92400E':'#991B1B'};border-radius:999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px">${icon('checkCircle',12)} ${bestPct}% · ${hist.tries}회</span>`
            : '';
          return `
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastRunner('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year},${sess})">
            제${sess}회 정기시험 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${badge}${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: 특정 (base, year, session)에 대한 모든 변종 exams (필터 적용)
function getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, variant){
  return getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant||'all')
    .filter(p => p.meta.year === year && p.meta.session === session);
}

// Step 4: 풀이 방식(과목별/전체/모의) 선택
// v4.35: 세션 진입 시 상선/어선 변종을 하나로 합쳐서 보여주고, 상단에 상선/어선 필터 추가.
//        필터를 바꿀 때마다 과목 체크박스/문제 수가 재계산된다.
function renderPastRunner(subjectId, baseShort, year, session){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year; pastSession = session;pastAllYears=false;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, 'all');
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // v4.45: 동적 variant 선택지 (navi: 상선/어선, engi5: 일반/국내한정)
  const vc = getVariantChoices(examsAll);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  // 현재 필터에 맞는 exams 만
  const exams = examsAll.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  // 과목별 수 집계(필터 적용)
  const subjCount = new Map();
  let totalQ = 0;
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCount.set(k, (subjCount.get(k)||0)+1);
    totalQ++;
  }));
  const subjects = [...subjCount.entries()];
  const allMockCount = subjects.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastRunnerVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year})">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 회차 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 제${session}회 ${label}</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${totalQ}문제 · ${subjects.length}과목</p>
    </div>
    ${showVariantFilter ? `
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px">${vc.title}</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">과목별 학습</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">선택한 과목만 풀이 · 여러 개 선택 가능</div>
      <div id="past-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjects.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;f

--- 3 @ 22226980 ---
:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastAllYearsSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastAllYearsSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastAllYearsSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastAllYearsSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">년도 선택 랜덤</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">원하는 년도만 골라서 랜덤 출제</div>
      <div id="past-yearcheck-list" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${yearList.map(([y,b])=>{
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-year-check" value="${y}" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${y}년</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${b.count}문제</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('study')">선택한 년도 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('mock')">선택한 년도 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">년도별 회차 선택</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${yearList.map(([y,b])=>`
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${y})">
            ${y}년 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${b.sessions.size}회차 · ${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경
function setPastYearPickVariant(v){
  pastYearPickVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort) renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면 과목 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastAllYearsSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
function togglePastYearChecks(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
async function startPastYearPickSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const years = [...document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check:checked')].map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
  if(!years.length){ showToast('년도를 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, pastVariant||'all')
    .filter(p => years.includes(p.meta.year));
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('해당 년도의 데이터가 없습니다.'); return; }
  const pool = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => pool.push({
    ...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  // 과목별 분리 후 모의시험 or 학습
  var finalPool;
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    var bySubj2 = new Map();
    pool.forEach(function(q){
      var k = q['과목']||'기타';
      if(!bySubj2.has(k)) bySubj2.set(k,[]);
      bySubj2.get(k).push(q);
    });
    finalPool = [];
    bySubj2.forEach(function(qs){
      finalPool.push.apply(finalPool, shuffle(qs.slice()).slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    finalPool = shuffle(finalPool);
  } else {
    finalPool = shuffle(pool.slice());
  }
  renderPastLoading('선택한 년도의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(years);
  pastAllYears=true;pastYear=null;pastSession=null;pastReturnView=null;
  pastQueue = finalPool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(finalPool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션 시작 — 과목 체크박스 + 변종 필터 기준
async function startPastAllYearsSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  const filterSet = new Set(picked);
  // 과목별로 모으기 (전 년도 전 회차)
  const bySubj = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push({...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort});
  }));
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('전체 년도 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(exams.map(p=>p.meta.year));
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;         // v4.44: 그만/결과 후 년도 선택으로 복귀하도록 플래그
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// v4.38: renderPastSubjectAggregate + setPastVariant + toggleAggSubjects + startPastAggregateSession 제거.
//        동일 기능(과목체크/상선어선 필터/학습·모의)을 renderPastRunner가 회차 단위로 완전 대체.

// Step 3: 회차 선택
// v4.35: 상선/어선 변종을 하나의 회차 버튼으로 통합(제N회 정기시험 × 1개).
//        상선/어선 구분은 다음 화면(runner)의 필터에서 처리.
function renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all')
    .filter(p=>p.meta.year===year);
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // 회차별 집계 (변종 합산: 제1회 상선+제1회 어선 → 한 행)
  const bySession = new Map(); // session -> {count, variants:Set}
  examsAll.forEach(p => {
    const s = p.meta.session;
    const b = bySession.get(s) || {count:0, variants:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    const vl = variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort);
    if(vl) b.variants.add(vl);
    bySession.set(s, b);
  });
  const sessionList = [...bySession.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 년도 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 ${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 회차 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${sessionList.length}회차 · 총 ${examsAll.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${sessionList.map(([sess,b])=>{
          // v4.46: 풀이 기록 뱃지 (최고 정답률 · 시도 횟수)
          const hist = getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, sess);
          const bestPct = hist ? Math.round(hist.best*100) : null;
          const badge = hist
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${bestPct>=80?'#D1FAE5':bestPct>=60?'#FEF3C7':'#FEE2E2'};color:${bestPct>=80?'#065F46':bestPct>=60?'#92400E':'#991B1B'};border-radius:999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px">${icon('checkCircle',12)} ${bestPct}% · ${hist.tries}회</span>`
            : '';
          return `
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastRunner('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year},${sess})">
            제${sess}회 정기시험 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${badge}${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: 특정 (base, year, session)에 대한 모든 변종 exams (필터 적용)
function getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, variant){
  return getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant||'all')
    .filter(p => p.meta.year === year && p.meta.session === session);
}

// Step 4: 풀이 방식(과목별/전체/모의) 선택
// v4.35: 세션 진입 시 상선/어선 변종을 하나로 합쳐서 보여주고, 상단에 상선/어선 필터 추가.

function renderPastRunner(subjectId, baseShort, year, session){

  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year; pastSession = session;pastAllYears=false;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, 'all');
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();

  const vc = getVariantChoices(examsAll);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  // 현재 필터에 맞는 exams 만
  const exams = examsAll.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  // 과목별 수 집계(필터 적용)
  const subjCount = new Map();
