  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCount.set(k, (subjCount.get(k)||0)+1);
    totalQ++;
  }));
  const subjects = [...subjCount.entries()];
  const allMockCount = subjects.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastRunnerVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year})">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 회차 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 제${session}회 ${label}</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${totalQ}문제 · ${subjects.length}과목</p>
    </div>
    ${showVariantFilter ? `
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px">${vc.title}</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">과목별 학습</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">선택한 과목만 풀이 · 여러 개 선택 가능</div>
      <div id="past-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjects.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">전체 풀기</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('study','all')">전체 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('mock','all')">모의시험 (${allMockCount}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 

--- 4 @ 22228608 ---
items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-year-check" value="${y}" style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${y}년</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${b.count}문제</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastYearChecks(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('study')">선택한 년도 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastYearPickSession('mock')">선택한 년도 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">년도별 회차 선택</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${yearList.map(([y,b])=>`
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${y})">
            ${y}년 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${b.sessions.size}회차 · ${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경
function setPastYearPickVariant(v){
  pastYearPickVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort) renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면 과목 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastAllYearsSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
function togglePastYearChecks(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
async function startPastYearPickSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const years = [...document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check:checked')].map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
  if(!years.length){ showToast('년도를 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, pastVariant||'all')
    .filter(p => years.includes(p.meta.year));
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('해당 년도의 데이터가 없습니다.'); return; }
  const pool = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => pool.push({
    ...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  // 과목별 분리 후 모의시험 or 학습
  var finalPool;
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    var bySubj2 = new Map();
    pool.forEach(function(q){
      var k = q['과목']||'기타';
      if(!bySubj2.has(k)) bySubj2.set(k,[]);
      bySubj2.get(k).push(q);
    });
    finalPool = [];
    bySubj2.forEach(function(qs){
      finalPool.push.apply(finalPool, shuffle(qs.slice()).slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    finalPool = shuffle(finalPool);
  } else {
    finalPool = shuffle(pool.slice());
  }
  renderPastLoading('선택한 년도의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(years);
  pastAllYears=true;pastYear=null;pastSession=null;pastReturnView=null;
  pastQueue = finalPool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(finalPool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션 시작 — 과목 체크박스 + 변종 필터 기준
async function startPastAllYearsSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  const filterSet = new Set(picked);
  // 과목별로 모으기 (전 년도 전 회차)
  const bySubj = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push({...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort});
  }));
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('전체 년도 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(exams.map(p=>p.meta.year));
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;         // v4.44: 그만/결과 후 년도 선택으로 복귀하도록 플래그
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// v4.38: renderPastSubjectAggregate + setPastVariant + toggleAggSubjects + startPastAggregateSession 제거.
//        동일 기능(과목체크/상선어선 필터/학습·모의)을 renderPastRunner가 회차 단위로 완전 대체.

// Step 3: 회차 선택
// v4.35: 상선/어선 변종을 하나의 회차 버튼으로 통합(제N회 정기시험 × 1개).
//        상선/어선 구분은 다음 화면(runner)의 필터에서 처리.
function renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all')
    .filter(p=>p.meta.year===year);
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // 회차별 집계 (변종 합산: 제1회 상선+제1회 어선 → 한 행)
  const bySession = new Map(); // session -> {count, variants:Set}
  examsAll.forEach(p => {
    const s = p.meta.session;
    const b = bySession.get(s) || {count:0, variants:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    const vl = variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort);
    if(vl) b.variants.add(vl);
    bySession.set(s, b);
  });
  const sessionList = [...bySession.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 년도 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 ${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 회차 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${sessionList.length}회차 · 총 ${examsAll.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${sessionList.map(([sess,b])=>{
          // v4.46: 풀이 기록 뱃지 (최고 정답률 · 시도 횟수)
          const hist = getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, sess);
          const bestPct = hist ? Math.round(hist.best*100) : null;
          const badge = hist
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${bestPct>=80?'#D1FAE5':bestPct>=60?'#FEF3C7':'#FEE2E2'};color:${bestPct>=80?'#065F46':bestPct>=60?'#92400E':'#991B1B'};border-radius:999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px">${icon('checkCircle',12)} ${bestPct}% · ${hist.tries}회</span>`
            : '';
          return `
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastRunner('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year},${sess})">
            제${sess}회 정기시험 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${badge}${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: 특정 (base, year, session)에 대한 모든 변종 exams (필터 적용)
function getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, variant){
  return getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant||'all')
    .filter(p => p.meta.year === year && p.meta.session === session);
}

// Step 4: 풀이 방식(과목별/전체/모의) 선택
// v4.35: 세션 진입 시 상선/어선 변종을 하나로 합쳐서 보여주고, 상단에 상선/어선 필터 추가.
//        필터를 바꿀 때마다 과목 체크박스/문제 수가 재계산된다.
function renderPastRunner(subjectId, baseShort, year, session){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year; pastSession = session;pastAllYears=false;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, 'all');
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // v4.45: 동적 variant 선택지 (navi: 상선/어선, engi5: 일반/국내한정)
  const vc = getVariantChoices(examsAll);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  // 현재 필터에 맞는 exams 만
  const exams = examsAll.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  // 과목별 수 집계(필터 적용)
  const subjCount = new Map();
  let totalQ = 0;
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCount.set(k, (subjCount.get(k)||0)+1);
    totalQ++;
  }));
  const subjects = [...subjCount.entries()];
  const allMockCount = subjects.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastRunnerVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year})">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 회차 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 제${session}회 ${label}</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${totalQ}문제 · ${subjects.length}과목</p>
    </div>
    ${showVariantFilter ? `
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px">${vc.title}</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">과목별 학습</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">선택한 과목만 풀이 · 여러 개 선택 가능</div>
      <div id="past-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjects.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">전체 풀기</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('study','all')">전체 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('mock','all')">모의시험 (${allMockCount}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pa

--- 5 @ 22230361 ---

            ${y}년 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${b.sessions.size}회차 · ${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경
function setPastYearPickVariant(v){
  pastYearPickVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort) renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
}
// v4.44: 년도 선택 화면 과목 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastAllYearsSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
function togglePastYearChecks(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}
async function startPastYearPickSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const years = [...document.querySelectorAll('.past-year-check:checked')].map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
  if(!years.length){ showToast('년도를 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, pastVariant||'all')
    .filter(p => years.includes(p.meta.year));
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('해당 년도의 데이터가 없습니다.'); return; }
  const pool = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => pool.push({
    ...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  // 과목별 분리 후 모의시험 or 학습
  var finalPool;
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    var bySubj2 = new Map();
    pool.forEach(function(q){
      var k = q['과목']||'기타';
      if(!bySubj2.has(k)) bySubj2.set(k,[]);
      bySubj2.get(k).push(q);
    });
    finalPool = [];
    bySubj2.forEach(function(qs){
      finalPool.push.apply(finalPool, shuffle(qs.slice()).slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    finalPool = shuffle(finalPool);
  } else {
    finalPool = shuffle(pool.slice());
  }
  renderPastLoading('선택한 년도의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(years);
  pastAllYears=true;pastYear=null;pastSession=null;pastReturnView=null;
  pastQueue = finalPool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(finalPool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션 시작 — 과목 체크박스 + 변종 필터 기준
async function startPastAllYearsSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-allyears-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  const filterSet = new Set(picked);
  // 과목별로 모으기 (전 년도 전 회차)
  const bySubj = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push({...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort});
  }));
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('전체 년도 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(exams.map(p=>p.meta.year));
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;         // v4.44: 그만/결과 후 년도 선택으로 복귀하도록 플래그
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// v4.38: renderPastSubjectAggregate + setPastVariant + toggleAggSubjects + startPastAggregateSession 제거.
//        동일 기능(과목체크/상선어선 필터/학습·모의)을 renderPastRunner가 회차 단위로 완전 대체.

// Step 3: 회차 선택
// v4.35: 상선/어선 변종을 하나의 회차 버튼으로 통합(제N회 정기시험 × 1개).
//        상선/어선 구분은 다음 화면(runner)의 필터에서 처리.
function renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all')
    .filter(p=>p.meta.year===year);
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // 회차별 집계 (변종 합산: 제1회 상선+제1회 어선 → 한 행)
  const bySession = new Map(); // session -> {count, variants:Set}
  examsAll.forEach(p => {
    const s = p.meta.session;
    const b = bySession.get(s) || {count:0, variants:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    const vl = variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort);
    if(vl) b.variants.add(vl);
    bySession.set(s, b);
  });
  const sessionList = [...bySession.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 년도 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 ${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 회차 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${sessionList.length}회차 · 총 ${examsAll.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${sessionList.map(([sess,b])=>{
          // v4.46: 풀이 기록 뱃지 (최고 정답률 · 시도 횟수)
          const hist = getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, sess);
          const bestPct = hist ? Math.round(hist.best*100) : null;
          const badge = hist
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${bestPct>=80?'#D1FAE5':bestPct>=60?'#FEF3C7':'#FEE2E2'};color:${bestPct>=80?'#065F46':bestPct>=60?'#92400E':'#991B1B'};border-radius:999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px">${icon('checkCircle',12)} ${bestPct}% · ${hist.tries}회</span>`
            : '';
          return `
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastRunner('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year},${sess})">
            제${sess}회 정기시험 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${badge}${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: 특정 (base, year, session)에 대한 모든 변종 exams (필터 적용)
function getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, variant){
  return getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant||'all')
    .filter(p => p.meta.year === year && p.meta.session === session);
}

// Step 4: 풀이 방식(과목별/전체/모의) 선택
// v4.35: 세션 진입 시 상선/어선 변종을 하나로 합쳐서 보여주고, 상단에 상선/어선 필터 추가.
//        필터를 바꿀 때마다 과목 체크박스/문제 수가 재계산된다.
function renderPastRunner(subjectId, baseShort, year, session){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year; pastSession = session;pastAllYears=false;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, 'all');
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // v4.45: 동적 variant 선택지 (navi: 상선/어선, engi5: 일반/국내한정)
  const vc = getVariantChoices(examsAll);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  // 현재 필터에 맞는 exams 만
  const exams = examsAll.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  // 과목별 수 집계(필터 적용)
  const subjCount = new Map();
  let totalQ = 0;
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCount.set(k, (subjCount.get(k)||0)+1);
    totalQ++;
  }));
  const subjects = [...subjCount.entries()];
  const allMockCount = subjects.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastRunnerVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year})">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 회차 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 제${session}회 ${label}</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${totalQ}문제 · ${subjects.length}과목</p>
    </div>
    ${showVariantFilter ? `
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px">${vc.title}</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">과목별 학습</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">선택한 과목만 풀이 · 여러 개 선택 가능</div>
      <div id="past-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjects.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">전체 풀기</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('study','all')">전체 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('mock','all')">모의시험 (${allMockCount}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]!==null)return;
  pastAnswers[pastIdx] = i;
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastNext(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]===null)return;
  if(pastIdx+1 >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  pastIdx++;
  renderPastCard();
}

function renderPastResult(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  currentMode='past-result';
  clearPastProgress();
  const total = pastQueue.length;
  const isNavi3Practice = pastReturnView === 'navi3-practice';
  const analysisGradeLabel = ((n3aData()&&n3aData().meta&&n3aData().meta.gradeLabel)||'항해사').replace(/\s*\(상선\)\s*/g,'');
  let correct = 0;
  const wrongs = [];
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) correct++;
    else wrongs.push({q, i, ans: pastAnswers[i]});
  });
  const pct = total ? Math.round(100*correct/total) : 0;
  // v4.46: 오답 자동 누적 + 정답은 오답노트에서 자동 제거 + 풀이 기록 저장 (단일 회차만)
  wrongs.forEach(({q,ans}) => addPastWrong(q, ans));
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) removePastWrong(pqid(q));
  });
  if(!pastAllYears && !pastReturnView && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession){
    recordPastSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, correct, total, pastMode);
  }
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now()-pastStartedAt)/1000);
  const mm = Math.floor(elapsed/60), ss = elapsed%60;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===pastSubjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px;margi

--- 6 @ 22233029 ---
주세요.'); return; }
  const curV = pastYearPickVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsByBase(pastBaseShort, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  const filterSet = new Set(picked);
  // 과목별로 모으기 (전 년도 전 회차)
  const bySubj = new Map();
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push({...q, _year: p.meta.year, _session: p.meta.session, '회차': p.meta.session, _short: p.meta.gradeShort});
  }));
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('전체 년도 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(exams.map(p=>p.meta.year));
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;         // v4.44: 그만/결과 후 년도 선택으로 복귀하도록 플래그
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

// v4.38: renderPastSubjectAggregate + setPastVariant + toggleAggSubjects + startPastAggregateSession 제거.
//        동일 기능(과목체크/상선어선 필터/학습·모의)을 renderPastRunner가 회차 단위로 완전 대체.

// Step 3: 회차 선택
// v4.35: 상선/어선 변종을 하나의 회차 버튼으로 통합(제N회 정기시험 × 1개).
//        상선/어선 구분은 다음 화면(runner)의 필터에서 처리.
function renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year){
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, 'all')
    .filter(p=>p.meta.year===year);
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastYearPick(subjectId, baseShort); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // 회차별 집계 (변종 합산: 제1회 상선+제1회 어선 → 한 행)
  const bySession = new Map(); // session -> {count, variants:Set}
  examsAll.forEach(p => {
    const s = p.meta.session;
    const b = bySession.get(s) || {count:0, variants:new Set()};
    b.count += p.questions.length;
    const vl = variantLabel(p.meta.gradeShort);
    if(vl) b.variants.add(vl);
    bySession.set(s, b);
  });
  const sessionList = [...bySession.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastYearPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}')">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 년도 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 ${label} <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--textDim)">· 회차 선택</span></h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${sessionList.length}회차 · 총 ${examsAll.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.questions.length,0)}문제</p>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">
        ${sessionList.map(([sess,b])=>{
          // v4.46: 풀이 기록 뱃지 (최고 정답률 · 시도 횟수)
          const hist = getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, sess);
          const bestPct = hist ? Math.round(hist.best*100) : null;
          const badge = hist
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${bestPct>=80?'#D1FAE5':bestPct>=60?'#FEF3C7':'#FEE2E2'};color:${bestPct>=80?'#065F46':bestPct>=60?'#92400E':'#991B1B'};border-radius:999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px">${icon('checkCircle',12)} ${bestPct}% · ${hist.tries}회</span>`
            : '';
          return `
          <button class="btn btn-outline" style="text-align:left;padding:20px 18px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};font-size:17px;font-weight:800" onclick="renderPastRunner('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year},${sess})">
            제${sess}회 정기시험 <span style="float:right;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;line-height:28px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px">${badge}${b.count}문제 ${icon('chevronRight',14)}</span>
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: 특정 (base, year, session)에 대한 모든 변종 exams (필터 적용)
function getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, variant){
  return getPastExamsByBase(baseShort, variant||'all')
    .filter(p => p.meta.year === year && p.meta.session === session);
}

// Step 4: 풀이 방식(과목별/전체/모의) 선택
// v4.35: 세션 진입 시 상선/어선 변종을 하나로 합쳐서 보여주고, 상단에 상선/어선 필터 추가.
//        필터를 바꿀 때마다 과목 체크박스/문제 수가 재계산된다.
function renderPastRunner(subjectId, baseShort, year, session){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  pastReturnView=null;
  pastSubjectId = subjectId; pastBaseShort = baseShort; pastYear = year; pastSession = session;pastAllYears=false;
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(subjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const examsAll = getPastExamsForSession(baseShort, year, session, 'all');
  if(!examsAll.length){ renderPastSessionPick(subjectId, baseShort, year); return; }
  const label = BASE_GRADE_LABEL[baseShort] || (examsAll[0].meta.gradeLabel || baseShort).replace(/\s*\(.+?\)\s*/g,'').trim();
  // v4.45: 동적 variant 선택지 (navi: 상선/어선, engi5: 일반/국내한정)
  const vc = getVariantChoices(examsAll);
  const showVariantFilter = vc.choices.length > 0;
  if(!showVariantFilter) pastVariant = 'all';
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  // 현재 필터에 맞는 exams 만
  const exams = examsAll.filter(p => variantMatches(p.meta.gradeShort, curV));
  // 과목별 수 집계(필터 적용)
  const subjCount = new Map();
  let totalQ = 0;
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => {
    const k = q['과목'] || '기타';
    subjCount.set(k, (subjCount.get(k)||0)+1);
    totalQ++;
  }));
  const subjects = [...subjCount.entries()];
  const allMockCount = subjects.length * PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT;
  const variantBtn = (v, name) => `
    <button class="btn ${curV===v?'btn-accent':'btn-outline'}" style="flex:1;font-size:13px;padding:10px;border-color:${gColor};${curV===v?`background:${gColor}`:`color:${gColor}`}" onclick="setPastRunnerVariant('${v}')">${name}</button>`;
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:24px;margin-bottom:20px">
      <button type="button" class="text-nav" onclick="renderPastSessionPick('${subjectId}','${baseShort}',${year})">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 회차 선택으로</button>
      <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px 0;color:${gColor}">${year}년 제${session}회 ${label}</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim)">${totalQ}문제 · ${subjects.length}과목</p>
    </div>
    ${showVariantFilter ? `
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:8px">${vc.title}</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        ${variantBtn('all','전체')}${vc.choices.map(c => variantBtn(c, c)).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
    <div class="card">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px">과목별 학습</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:12px">선택한 과목만 풀이 · 여러 개 선택 가능</div>
      <div id="past-subject-checks" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px">
        ${subjects.map(([subj,cnt])=>{
          const safeSubj = String(subj).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
          return `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff">
            <input type="checkbox" class="past-subj-check" value="${safeSubj}" checked style="width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:${gColor}">
            <span style="flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${subj}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim)">${cnt}문</span>
          </label>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(true)">전체 선택</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:110px;font-size:12px;padding:8px" onclick="togglePastSubjects(false)">전체 해제</button>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};background:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('study')">선택한 과목 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSubjectSession('mock')">선택한 과목 모의시험 (과목당 ${PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">전체 풀기</div>
      <div class="flex gap8" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('study','all')">전체 학습</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;border-color:${gColor};color:${gColor};padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800" onclick="startPastSession('mock','all')">모의시험 (${allMockCount}문)</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// v4.35: runner 화면의 상선/어선 필터 변경 → 재렌더
function setPastRunnerVariant(v){
  pastVariant = v;
  if(pastSubjectId && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession)
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
}

// v4.38: 그만/다시 시 복귀 — runner 한 종류만 남아 단순화.
// v4.44: 전 년도 통합 세션(pastAllYears=true)이면 년도 선택 화면으로 복귀.
function goBackFromPastSession(){
  currentMode="past-menu";
  if(!pastSubjectId||!pastBaseShort){renderSubjectSelect();return;}
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-practice'){
    const target=navi3PracticeReturn;
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(target&&target.kind==='topic'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic',target.id);
    else if(target&&target.kind==='repeat'&&target.id)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat',target.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastReturnView==='navi3-analysis'){
    pastReturnView=null;
    currentMode='analysis';
    if(navi3AnalysisDetail)renderNavi3FrequencyDetail(navi3AnalysisDetail.kind,navi3AnalysisDetail.id);
    else renderNavi3Frequency();
    return;
  }
  if(pastAllYears){
    pastAllYears = false;
    renderPastYearPick(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort);
  } else {
    renderPastRunner(pastSubjectId, pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession);
  }
}

// 체크박스 전체 선택/해제
function togglePastSubjects(on){
  document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = on; });
}

// 체크된 과목들로 세션 시작 (mode 'study'|'mock')
function startPastSubjectSession(mode){
  mode = mode || 'study';
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.past-subj-check');
  const picked = [...checks].filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!picked.length){ showToast('과목을 1개 이상 선택해주세요.'); return; }
  startPastSession(mode, picked);
}

// 실제 세션 시작 (현재 선택된 base/year/session/variant 기준)
// v4.35: 상선+어선 변종을 모두 합쳐서 풀로 구성 (variant 필터 적용).
//        mock 모드일 때는 "과목당 PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT 문"으로 샘플.
//        subjFilter: 'all' | string (단일과목) | string[] (다중과목)
async function startPastSession(mode, subjFilter){
  const curV = pastVariant || 'all';
  const exams = getPastExamsForSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, curV);
  if(!exams.length){ showToast('데이터를 찾을 수 없습니다.'); return; }
  if(NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(pastBaseShort)&&Number(pastYear)>=2026){
    renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 신출·기출 이력을 불러오는 중입니다`);
    try{await ensureNavi3FrequencyData(pastBaseShort)}catch(error){console.warn('[v5.03] 출제 이력 로드 실패:',error)}
  }
  // 모든 변종 문제 합치기
  const allQuestions = [];
  exams.forEach(p => p.questions.forEach(q => allQuestions.push({
    ...q, _year: pastYear, _session: pastSession, '회차': pastSession, _short: p.meta.gradeShort
  })));
  // 과목 필터 결정
  let filterSet;
  if(!subjFilter || subjFilter === 'all'){
    filterSet = new Set(allQuestions.map(q=>q['과목']));
  } else if(Array.isArray(subjFilter)){
    filterSet = new Set(subjFilter);
  } else {
    filterSet = new Set([subjFilter]);
  }
  // 과목별로 모으기
  const bySubj = new Map();
  allQuestions.forEach(q => {
    if(!filterSet.has(q['과목'])) return;
    if(!bySubj.has(q['과목'])) bySubj.set(q['과목'], []);
    bySubj.get(q['과목']).push(q);
  });
  let pool = [];
  if(mode === 'mock'){
    bySubj.forEach(arr => {
      const s = shuffle(arr.slice());
      pool.push(...s.slice(0, PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT));
    });
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  } else {
    bySubj.forEach(arr => pool.push(...arr));
    pool = shuffle(pool);
  }
  if(!pool.length){ showToast('해당 조건의 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading(`${pastYear}년 해설을 불러오는 중입니다`);
  await ensurePastExplainForYears([pastYear]);
  pastQueue = pool;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pool.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = mode;
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastChoiceText(value){
  return String(value==null?'':value).replace(/^\s*[㉮㉯㉰㉱㉴㉵]\s*/, '').trim();
}

function renderPastCard(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  savePastProgress();
  if(pastIdx < 0 || pastIdx >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx];
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const selected = pastAnswers[pastIdx];
  const showFeedback = pastMode === 'study' && selected !== null;
  const correct = q['정답'];
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  const marked = isPastMarked(q);  // v4.46: 북마크 상태
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:auto;font-size:13px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px" onclick="showConfirm('학습을 중단하시겠습니까?', goBackFromPastSession)">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 그만</button>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <button onclick="togglePastMark()" title="북마크 (B)" aria-label="북마크"
          style="background:${marked?'#FEF3C7':'transparent'};border:1.5px solid ${marked?'#F59E0B':'#E5E7EB'};border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:${marked?'#D97706':'#94A3B8'};font-weight:800">
          ${marked?icon('star',18):icon('starOutline',18)}
        </button>
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">
          ${pastMode==='mock'?'모의 ':''}${pastIdx+1} / ${pastQueue.length}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="tag" style="background:${gColor}20;color:${gColor};margin:0">${q._year}년</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#E0E7FF;color:#3730A3;margin:0">${q['회차']}회</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">${q['과목']}</span>
        <span class="tag" style="background:#F1F5F9;color:#475569;margin:0">Q${q['번호']}</span>
        ${n3aOriginTagHtml(q)}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:16px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
        ${q['선택지'].map((opt,i)=>{
          const isSelected = selected === i;
          const isCorrect = i === correct;
          let bg = '#fff', border = '#E5E7EB', col = 'var(--text)';
          if(showFeedback){
            if(isCorrect){ bg='#D1FAE5'; border='#10B981'; col='#065F46'; }
            else if(isSelected && !isCorrect){ bg='#FEE2E2'; border='#DC2626'; col='#991B1B'; }
          } else if(isSelected){
            bg = gColor+'15'; border = gColor; col = gColor;
          }
          return `<button onclick="choosePastAnswer(${i})" style="text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;background:${bg};border:2px solid ${border};border-radius:10px;color:${col};font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;cursor:${showFeedback?'default':'pointer'};transition:all 0.15s" ${showFeedback?'disabled':''}>
            <span style="font-weight:800;margin-right:8px">${optMarkers[i]}</span>${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(opt))}
          </button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFeedback ? `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:${selected===correct?'#ECFDF5':'#FEF2F2'};border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${selected===correct?'#065F46':'#991B1B'};text-align:center">
          ${selected===correct?icon('checkCircle',16)+' 정답':icon('xCircle',16)+' 오답 · 정답: '+optMarkers[correct]}
        </div>
        ${renderExplainBlock(q)}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
      ${pastIdx>0?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="pastIdx--;renderPastCard()">${icon('arrowLeft',14)} 이전</button>`:''}
      ${(selected!==null || pastMode==='mock')?`<button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:2" onclick="pastNext()">${pastIdx+1===pastQueue.length?'제출':'다음 '+icon('arrowRight',14)}</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:2;opacity:0.5" disabled>답을 선택하세요</button>`}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:10px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--textMuted)">
      단축키: <b>1~4</b> 답 선택 · <b>Enter</b>/<b>→</b> 다음 · <b>←</b> 이전 · <b>B</b> 북마크
    </div>
  `;
}

function choosePastAnswer(i){
  if(currentMode!=='past'||!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=pastQueue[pastIdx]['선택지'].length)return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]!==null)return;
  pastAnswers[pastIdx] = i;
  renderPastCard();
}

function pastNext(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  if(pastMode==='study'&&pastAnswers[pastIdx]===null)return;
  if(pastIdx+1 >= pastQueue.length){ renderPastResult(); return; }
  pastIdx++;
  renderPastCard();
}

function renderPastResult(){
  if(currentMode!=='past')return;
  currentMode='past-result';
  clearPastProgress();
  const total = pastQueue.length;
  const isNavi3Practice = pastReturnView === 'navi3-practice';
  const analysisGradeLabel = ((n3aData()&&n3aData().meta&&n3aData().meta.gradeLabel)||'항해사').replace(/\s*\(상선\)\s*/g,'');
  let correct = 0;
  const wrongs = [];
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) correct++;
    else wrongs.push({q, i, ans: pastAnswers[i]});
  });
  const pct = total ? Math.round(100*correct/total) : 0;
  // v4.46: 오답 자동 누적 + 정답은 오답노트에서 자동 제거 + 풀이 기록 저장 (단일 회차만)
  wrongs.forEach(({q,ans}) => addPastWrong(q, ans));
  pastQueue.forEach((q,i)=>{
    if(pastAnswers[i] === q['정답']) removePastWrong(pqid(q));
  });
  if(!pastAllYears && !pastReturnView && pastBaseShort && pastYear && pastSession){
    recordPastSession(pastBaseShort, pastYear, pastSession, correct, total, pastMode);
  }
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now()-pastStartedAt)/1000);
  const mm = Math.floor(elapsed/60), ss = elapsed%60;
  const s = SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===pastSubjectId);
  const g = SUBJECT_GROUPS.find(x=>x.ids.includes(pastSubjectId));
  const gColor = g?g.color:'#1E40AF';
  const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
  app.innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding-top:20px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px;margin-bottom:6px">${pastMode==='mock'?'모의시험 결과':'학습 결과'}</div>
      <h1 style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;margin:0;color:${gColor}">${isNavi3Practice?`${escapeHtml(analysisGradeLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(navi3PracticeLabel)}`:`${s?s.name:''} · 필기 기출`}</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;border-left:3px solid ${gColor}">
      <div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:900;color:${gColor};line-height:1">${correct} / ${total}</div>
      <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-top:6px">정답률 ${pct}%</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--textDim);margin-top:4px">소요시간 ${mm}분 ${ss}초</div>
    </div>
    ${wrongs.length ? `
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:10px">오답 ${wrongs.length}개</div>
        ${wrongs.slice(0,30).map(({q,i,ans})=>`
          <div style="padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #F1F5F9">
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:4px">${q._year}년 ${q['회차']}회 · ${q['과목']} · Q${q['번호']}</div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#DC2626">내 답: ${ans!==null?optMarkers[ans]+' '+escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][ans])):'(미응답)'}</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:#059669">정답: ${optMarkers[q['정답']]} ${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][q['정답']]))}</div>
            ${renderExplainBlock(q,{compact:true})}
          </div>
        `).join('')}
        ${wrongs.length>30?`<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted);margin-top:8px;text-align:center">... 외 ${wrongs.length-30}개 (상위 30개만 표시)</div>`:''}
      </div>
    ` : `
      <div class="card" style="text-align:center;background:#D1FAE5">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#065F46">모두 정답</div>
      </div>
    `}
    ${wrongs.length?'<button class="btn btn-green mb12" onclick="retryPastSessionWrongs()">이번 오답만 다시 풀기 ('+wrongs.length+'문제)</button>':''}
    <div class="flex gap8" style="margin-top:12px">
      ${isNavi3Practice?`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">빈출 분석으로</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="restartNavi3FrequencyPractice()">같은 문제 다시 풀기</button>`:`<button class="btn btn-outline" style="flex:1" onclick="goBackFromPastSession()">${pastAllYears?'다시 선택':'이 회차 다시'}</button><button class="btn btn-accent" style="flex:1" onclick="pickPrepType(pastSubjectId)">과목 홈</button>`}
    </div>
  `;
}

function escapeHtml(s){
  return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c)

--- 7 @ 22483536 ---
ound:#FEF2F2;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;color:#991B1B">복습 예정 문제가 하루 상한을 넘어 ${assignment.dueOverflow}문제가 다음 배정으로 이월됩니다.</div>`:''}
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--textDim);line-height:1.6;margin-top:10px">완료 조건: 정답 + ‘확실히 안다’. 오답 또는 ‘애매·찍음’은 오늘 미해결로 남습니다. 첫 통과 후 다른 날 다시 정답+확실이면 숙달됩니다.</div>
        <button class="btn btn-accent" style="width:100%;margin-top:12px;background:#7C3AED;border-color:#7C3AED" ${unresolved.length?'':'disabled'} onclick="startNavigatorPassPlanToday()">${unresolved.length?`오늘 숙제 시작 · ${unresolved.length}문제`:'오늘 숙제 완료'}</button>
