let visualDrillIndex=0;
let visualDrillRevealed=false;

function visualPolar(cx,cy,r,bearing){
  const angle=(bearing-90)*Math.PI/180;
  return {x:cx+r*Math.cos(angle),y:cy+r*Math.sin(angle)};
}
function visualArcPath(cx,cy,r,startBearing,endBearing){
  const start=visualPolar(cx,cy,r,startBearing);
  const end=visualPolar(cx,cy,r,endBearing);
  const span=endBearing-startBearing;
  return `M ${start.x.toFixed(2)} ${start.y.toFixed(2)} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${span>180?1:0} 1 ${end.x.toFixed(2)} ${end.y.toFixed(2)}`;
}

  if(id==='signal-o'){
    svg=`<svg viewBox="0 0 320 190" role="img" aria-label="국제신호기 식별 도판" style="width:100%;max-width:360px;displ

===== pastQuestions count=2 =====

--- 1 @ 22197206 ---
><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--textDim)">최근출제</span></div></div>${n3aTimeline(hits)}</div>
    ${latestHit?`<button type="button" class="n3a-practice-btn" style="width:100%;margin:0 0 16px" onclick="openNavi3FrequencyQuestion('${group.id}',${latestHit.year},${latestHit.session},${latestHit.number})">${icon('arrowRight',16)} 최신 출제 문제 바로 풀기</button>`:''}
    <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;margin:0 2px 10px">출제 이력</div><div class="n3a-list">${[...hits].sort((a,b)=>b.year-a.year||b.session-a.session).map(hit=>`<button type="button" class="n3a-row" onclick="openNavi3FrequencyQuestion('${group.id}',${hit.year},${hit.session},${hit.number})"><span class="n3a-rank">${hit.session}</span><span><span class="n3a-row-title">${hit.year}년 제${hit.session}회 정기시험</span><span class="n3a-row-meta">${escapeHtml(group.subject)} · Q${hit.number} · 문제와 해설 보기</span></span><span class="n3a-row-count">${icon('chevronRight',20)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

async function openNavi3FrequencyQuestion(groupId,year,session,number){
  renderPastLoading(`${year}년 제${session}회 문제를 불러오는 중입니다`);
  try{
    const gradeId=naviAnalysisSubjectId;
    if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(gradeId))await ensurePastDataForSubject(gradeId);
    const exam=getPastExam(gradeId,year,session);
    const data=n3aData();
    const group=data&&(data.groups||[]).find(item=>item.id===groupId);
    const question=exam&&group&&exam.questions.find(item=>item['과목']===group.subject&&Number(item['번호'])===Number(number)&&n3aNormalizeQuestion(item['문제'])===group.normalized);
    if(!question)throw new Error('해당 기출문제를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    await ensurePastExplainForYears([year]);
    pastSubjectId=gradeId;pastBaseShort=gradeId;pastYear=year;pastSession=session;pastVariant='상선';
    pastQueue=[{...question,_year:year,_session:session,'회차':session,_short:gradeId}];
    pastIdx=0;pastAnswers=[null];pastMode='study';pastStartedAt=Date.now();pastAllYears=false;pastReturnView='navi3-analysis';
    navi3AnalysisDetail={kind:'repeat',id:groupId};currentMode='past';renderPastCard();
  }catch(error){
    app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px;text-align:center;border-left:4px solid #DC2626"><div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#991B1B">문제를 열지 못했습니다</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin:7px 0 15px">${escapeHtml(error.message||String(error))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat','${groupId}')">상세로 돌아가기</button></div>`;
  }
}

function n3aMatchTopic(subject,question){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return null;
  const text=String(question||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
  const rule=(data.rules||[]).find(item=>item.subject===subject&&new RegExp(item.pattern,'i').test(text));
  return rule&&(data.topics||[]).find(topic=>topic.id===rule.id);
}
function renderNavi3OralLink(question){
  if(!currentSubject||!NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(currentSubject.id)||!n3aData(currentSubject.id)||!question)return '';
  const topic=n3aMatchTopic(question.category,question.q);if(!topic)return '';
  const data=n3aData(currentSubject.id),yearSet=new Set((data.meta.years||[]).map(Number)),hits=n3aFilteredHits(topic.hits,yearSet);
  const sessions=new Set(hits.map(n3aSessionKey)).size,score=n3aTopicScore(hits,yearSet,n3aAllSessionKeys(data,yearSet).size),tier=n3aTier(score);
  return `<button type="button" class="n3a-mini-link" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer" onclick="renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic','${topic.id}')"><span style="font-weight:900">필기 연계 · ${escapeHtml(topic.label)}</span><br>${sessions}개 회차 · ${hits.length}문항 · <span class="n3a-score ${tier.cls}" style="margin-top:5px">${tier.label}</span></button>`;
}

// 필기시험 세션 상태
let pastQueue=[], pastIdx=0, pastAnswers=[], pastMode='study', pastStartedAt=0;
let pastSubjectId=null, pastYear=null, pastSession=null;
let pastBaseShort=null;            // v4.34: 현재 선택된 base 코드(navi3 등)
let pastVariant='all';             // v4.34: 'all'|'상선'|'어선'
let pastYearPickVariant='all';     // v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 (전 년도 모의/학습용)
let pastAllYears=false;            // v4.44: true면 전 년도 통합 세션 — goBackFromPastSession이 년도 선택으로 복귀
let pastReturnView=null;            // v4.97: 빈출 분석에서 연 기출문제의 복귀 화면
const PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT = 25;  // v4.34: 모의시험 = 선택 과목 × 25문

// ── v4.81: 기본 해설과 사용자 해설을 분리한 레지스트리 ──
// 외부 past-explain-{year}.js의 검수된 기본 해설만 표시합니다.
window.MD_EXPLAIN = window.MD_EXPLAIN || {};
function pastQuestionsMatch(a,b){
  return !!a && !!b &&
    a['문제']===b['문제'] &&
    a['정답']===b['정답'] &&
    JSON.stringify(a['선택지']||[])===JSON.stringify(b['선택지']||[]);
}
function getExplainIds(q){
  const exactId=pqid(q);
  const short=q._short||q._gradeShort||'?';
  const baseShort=baseFromShort(short);
  if(baseShort===short || !q._year) return [exactId];

  // 상선/어선 변종의 문제가 실제로 같을 때만 공통(base) 해설을 재사용합니다.
  const session=q._session||q['회차'];
  const baseExam=window.MD_PAST && window.MD_PAST[`${q._year}-${baseShort}-${session}`];
  const baseQuestion=baseExam && baseExam.questions.find(item=>
    item['과목']===q['과목'] && String(item['번호'])===String(q['번호'])
  );
  if(!pastQuestionsMatch(q,baseQuestion)) return [exactId];
  const baseId=`${q._year}|${baseShort}|${session||'?'}|${q['과목']||'?'}|${q['번호']||'?'}`;
  return exactId===baseId ? [exactId] : [exactId,baseId];
}
function getExplain(q){
  const ids=getExplainIds(q);
  const baseId=ids.find(id=>window.MD_EXPLAIN && window.MD_EXPLAIN[id]);
  const e = baseId && window.MD_EXPLAIN[baseId];
  if(!e) return null;
  // html 필드 단일 형식 우선 지원
  if(e.html){
    return { html: e.html, loading: !!e.loading };
  }
  // 폴백: 기존 분리 필드 형식 (해설/용어/키포인트)
  return {
    해설: e['해설'] || e.explain || '',
    용어: e['용어'] || e.terms || {},
    키포인트: e['키포인트'] || e.keypoints || [],
  };
}
// 기본 해설도 허용 태그/스타일만 남겨 innerHTML 주입을 차단합니다.
function sanitizeExplainHtml(input){
  if(typeof input!=='string') return '';
  const template=document.createElement('template');
  template.innerHTML=input;
  const allowedTags=new Set(['BR','STRONG','B','EM','I','SPAN','MARK','P','DIV','UL','OL','LI','TABLE','THEAD','TBODY','TR','TH','TD','HR','CODE']);
  const allowedStyles=new Set([
    'color','background','background-color','font-weight','font-size','font-style','line-height',
    'padding','padding-top','padding-right','padding-bottom','padding-left',
    'margin','margin-top','margin-right','margin-bottom','margin-left',
    'border','border-top','border-right','border-bottom','border-left','border-color','border-width','border-style','border-radius',
    'text-align','white-space','letter-spacing','text-decoration'
  ]);
  [...template.content.querySelectorAll('*')].forEach(el=>{
    if(!allowedTags.has(el.tagName)){
      el.replaceWith(...el.childNodes);
      return;
    }
    [...el.attributes].forEach(attr=>{
      if(attr.name.toLowerCase()!=='style') el.removeAttribute(attr.name);
    });
    if(el.hasAttribute('style')){
      [...el.style].forEach(prop=>{
        const value=el.style.getPropertyValue(prop);
        if(!allowedStyles.has(prop)||/url\s*\(|expression\s*\(|javascript:|@import/i.test(value)){
          el.style.removeProperty(prop);
        }
      });
      if(!el.getAttribute('style')) el.removeAttribute('style');
    }
  });
  return template.innerHTML;
}

// 해설 HTML 안에서 마크다운 일부(굵게/기울임)만 변환. <br>은 이미 들어있다고 가정.
function renderExplainMarkdown(s){
  if(typeof s !== 'string') return '';
  let html = sanitizeExplainHtml(s).replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  html = html.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');

  var sectionColors = {
    '용어 정리':   {bg:'#EFF6FF', border:'#BFDBFE', color:'#1D4ED8'},
    '정답 해설':   {bg:'#F0FDF4', border:'#BBF7D0', color:'#15803D'},
    '핵심 암기':   {bg:'#FFFBEB', border:'#FDE68A', color:'#B45309'},
    '지문 독해':   {bg:'#EFF6FF', border:'#BFDBFE', color:'#1D4ED8'},
    '보기 해석':   {bg:'#F5F3FF', border:'#DDD6FE', color:'#6D28D9'},
    '왜 정답인가?':{bg:'#F0FDF4', border:'#BBF7D0', color:'#15803D'}
  };

  var sectionPattern = /━━\s*([^━]+?)\s*━━/g;
  var parts = [];
  var lastIndex = 0;
  var match;
  while((match = sectionPattern.exec(html)) !== null){
    if(match.index > lastIndex) parts.push({type:'text', content: html.slice(lastIndex, match.index)});
    parts.push({type:'section', name: match[1].trim()});
    lastIndex = match.index + match[0].length;
  }
  if(lastIndex < html.length) parts.push({type:'text', content: html.slice(lastIndex)});

  var result = '';
  var currentSection = null;
  var currentContent = '';

  function flushSection(){
    if(currentSection === null){
      if(currentContent.trim()) result += '<div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.8;color:#0C4A6E">' + currentContent + '</div>';
    } else {
      var sc = sectionColors[currentSection] || {bg:'#F8FAFC', border:'#E2E8F0', color:'#334155'};
      result += '<div style="margin-bottom:10px;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px solid ' + sc.border + ';overflow:hidden">'
             + '<div style="padding:7px 13px;background:' + sc.border + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:' + sc.color + ';letter-spacing:0.5px">' + currentSection + '</div>'
             + '<div style="padding:11px 13px;background:' + sc.bg + ';font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.9;color:#1E293B">' + currentContent.replace(/^(<br>|\s)+/, '') + '</div>'
             + '</div>';
    }
    currentContent = '';
  }

  for(var i=0; i<parts.length; i++){
    var part = parts[i];
    if(part.type === 'section'){
      flushSection();
      currentSection = part.name;
    } else {
      currentContent += part.content;
    }
  }
  flushSection();
  return sanitizeExplainHtml(result);
}
function renderExplainBlock(q, opts){
  opts = opts || {};
  const e = getExplain(q);
  const compact = !!opts.compact;
  if(!e){
    return `<div style="margin-top:${compact?'8px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'10px':'12px'};background:#F8FAFC;border:1px dashed #CBD5E1;border-radius:10px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:var(--textMuted);text-align:center">해설 준비 중입니다</div>`;
  }
  // html 단일 필드 형식 — 정화 후 마크다운 일부 변환
  if(e.html){
    if(e.loading){
      return `<div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#FEF3C7;border:1px solid #FCD34D;border-radius:10px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#92400E;text-align:center">해설을 불러오는 중입니다</div>`;
    }
    return `
      <div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#F0F9FF;border:1px solid #BAE6FD;border-radius:10px">
        <div style="font-size:${compact?'13px':'14px'};line-height:1.75;color:#0C4A6E">${renderExplainMarkdown(e.html)}</div>
      </div>`;
  }
  // 폴백: 기존 분리 필드 렌더링
  const terms = e['용어'] && typeof e['용어']==='object' ? Object.entries(e['용어']) : [];
  const keys = Array.isArray(e['키포인트']) ? e['키포인트'] : [];
  return `
    <div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#F0F9FF;border:1px solid #BAE6FD;border-radius:10px">
      ${e['해설']?`<div style="font-size:${compact?'13px':'14px'};line-height:1.7;color:#0C4A6E;margin-bottom:${(terms.length||keys.length)?'10px':'0'};white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(e['해설'])}</div>`:''}
      ${terms.length?`
        <div style="margin-bottom:${keys.length?'10px':'0'}">
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1px;color:#075985;margin-bottom:4px">용어</div>
          ${terms.map(([t,d])=>`<div style="font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#0C4A6E;line-height:1.6"><b>${escapeHtml(t)}</b>: ${escapeHtml(d)}</div>`).join('')}
        </div>`:''}
      ${keys.length?`
        <div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1px;color:#075985;margin-bottom:4px">키포인트</div>
          <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:20px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#0C4A6E;line-height:1.6">
            ${keys.map(k=>`<li style="margin-bottom:2px">${escapeHtml(k)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>`:''}
    </div>`;
}
function explainCoverage(){
  // 진행도 계산용: 전체 문제 수 대비 해설 있는 문제 수
  if(!window.MD_PAST) return {done:0, total:0};
  let total=0, done=0;
  Object.values(window.MD_PAST).forEach(p=>{
    p.questions.forEach(q=>{
      total++;
      const normalized={...q,_year:p.meta.year,_session:p.meta.session,_short:p.meta.gradeShort,'회차':p.meta.session};
      const ids=getExplainIds(normalized);
      if(ids.some(id=>window.MD_EXPLAIN && window.MD_EXPLAIN[id])) done++;
    });
  });
  return {done, total};
}

// ── v4.46: 편의성 — 오답 노트 / 북마크 / 풀이 기록 ──
const PAST_WRONGS_KEY = 'md_past_wrongs_v1';
const PAST_MARKS_KEY  = 'md_past_marks_v1';
const PAST_HIST_KEY   = 'md_past_history_v1';
const PAST_WRONGS_MAX = 300;  // 최근 N개만 유지

// 문제 고유 ID: 년도|급코드|회차|과목|번호 — 세션 간 안정
function pqid(q){
  const short = q._short || q._gradeShort || '?';
  return `${q._year||'?'}|${short}|${q['회차']||'?'}|${q['과목']||'?'}|${q['번호']||'?'}`;
}
function pqSnapshot(q){
  return {
    _year: q._year, _short: q._short||q._gradeShort||'', _session: q._session,
    '회차': q['회차'], '과목': q['과목'], '번호': q['번호'],
    '문제': q['문제'], '선택지': q['선택지'], '정답': q['정답'],
  };
}
function normalizePastSavedMap(value){
  if(!value || typeof value!=='object' || Array.isArray(value)) return {map:{},changed:!!value};
  const map={};
  let changed=false;
  for(const [storedId,entry] of Object.entries(value)){
    if(!entry || typeof entry!=='object' || !entry.q){
      map[storedId]=entry;
      continue;
    }
    const exactId=pqid(entry.q);
    const normalized={...entry,id:exactId};
    const previous=map[exactId];
    if(!previous || Number(normalized.at||0)>=Number(previous.at||0)) map[exactId]=normalized;
    if(storedId!==exactId || entry.id!==exactId) changed=true;
  }
  return {map,changed};
}

// ── 오답 노트 ──
function loadPastWrongs(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(PAST_WRONGS_KEY);
    if(raw){
      const normalized=normalizePastSavedMap(JSON.parse(raw));
      if(normalized.changed) savePastWrongs(normalized.map);
      return normalized.map;
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[v4.81] 기출 오답 노트 로드 실패:',e)}
  return {};
}
function savePastWrongs(m){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_WRONGS_KEY,JSON.stringify(m),'기출 오답 노트');
}
function addPastWrong(q, myAns){
  const m = loadPastWrongs();
  const id = pqid(q);
  m[id] = { id, q: pqSnapshot(q), myAns, at: Date.now() };
  // 최대치 초과 시 오래된 것부터 제거
  const keys = Object.keys(m);
  if(keys.length > PAST_WRONGS_MAX){
    keys.sort((a,b)=>m[a].at - m[b].at);
    keys.slice(0, keys.length - PAST_WRONGS_MAX).forEach(k=>delete m[k]);
  }
  savePastWrongs(m);
}
function removePastWrong(id){
  const m = loadPastWrongs(); if(m[id]){ delete m[id]; savePastWrongs(m); }
}
function clearPastWrongs(){
  showConfirm('오답 노트를 모두 비우시겠습니까?',()=>{savePastWrongs({});renderPastWrongsList();});
}

// ── 북마크 ──
function loadPastMarks(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(PAST_MARKS_KEY);
    if(raw){
      const normalized=normalizePastSavedMap(JSON.parse(raw));
      if(normalized.changed) savePastMarks(normalized.map);
      return normalized.map;
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[v4.81] 기출 북마크 로드 실패:',e)}
  return {};
}
function savePastMarks(m){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_MARKS_KEY,JSON.stringify(m),'기출 북마크');
}
function isPastMarked(q){
  const m = loadPastMarks(); return !!m[pqid(q)];
}
function togglePastMark(){
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx]; if(!q) return;
  const m = loadPastMarks();
  const id = pqid(q);
  if(m[id]) delete m[id];
  else m[id] = { id, q: pqSnapshot(q), at: Date.now() };
  savePastMarks(m);
  renderPastCard();
}
function removePastMark(id){
  const m = loadPastMarks(); if(m[id]){ delete m[id]; savePastMarks(m); }
}

// ── 풀이 기록 ──
function loadPastHistory(){
  try{const r=localStorage.getItem(PAST_HIST_KEY); if(r) return JSON.parse(r);}catch(e){}
  return {};
}
function savePastHistory(h){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_HIST_KEY,JSON.stringify(h),'기출 풀이 기록');
}
function pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session){ return `${baseShort}|${year}|${session}`; }
function recordPastSession(baseShort, year, session, correct, total, mode){
  if(!baseShort || !year || !session || !total) return;
  const h = loadPastHistory();
  const key = pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session);
  const prev = h[key];
  const rate = correct/total;
  h[key] = {
    correct, total, mode, at: Date.now(),
    best: prev ? Math.max(prev.best||0, rate) : rate,
    tries: (prev?prev.tries:0)+1,
  };
  savePastHistory(h);
}
function getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, session){
  return loadPastHistory()[pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session)] || null;
}

async function openNavi3FrequencyQuestion(groupId,year,session,number){
  renderPastLoading(`${year}년 제${session}회 문제를 불러오는 중입니다`);
  try{
    const gradeId=naviAnalysisSubjectId;
    if(!pastDataSubjectsLoaded.has(gradeId))await ensurePastDataForSubject(gradeId);
    const exam=getPastExam(gradeId,year,session);
    const data=n3aData();
    const group=data&&(data.groups||[]).find(item=>item.id===groupId);
    const question=exam&&group&&exam.questions.find(item=>item['과목']===group.subject&&Number(item['번호'])===Number(number)&&n3aNormalizeQuestion(item['문제'])===group.normalized);
    if(!question)throw new Error('해당 기출문제를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    await ensurePastExplainForYears([year]);
    pastSubjectId=gradeId;pastBaseShort=gradeId;pastYear=year;pastSession=session;pastVariant='상선';
    pastQueue=[{...question,_year:year,_session:session,'회차':session,_short:gradeId}];
    pastIdx=0;pastAnswers=[null];pastMode='study';pastStartedAt=Date.now();pastAllYears=false;pastReturnView='navi3-analysis';
    navi3AnalysisDetail={kind:'repeat',id:groupId};currentMode='past';renderPastCard();
  }catch(error){
    app.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-top:30px;text-align:center;border-left:4px solid #DC2626"><div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:900;color:#991B1B">문제를 열지 못했습니다</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);margin:7px 0 15px">${escapeHtml(error.message||String(error))}</div><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('repeat','${groupId}')">상세로 돌아가기</button></div>`;
  }
}

function n3aMatchTopic(subject,question){
  const data=n3aData();if(!data)return null;
  const text=String(question||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
  const rule=(data.rules||[]).find(item=>item.subject===subject&&new RegExp(item.pattern,'i').test(text));
  return rule&&(data.topics||[]).find(topic=>topic.id===rule.id);
}
function renderNavi3OralLink(question){
  if(!currentSubject||!NAVI_ANALYSIS_GRADES.includes(currentSubject.id)||!n3aData(currentSubject.id)||!question)return '';
  const topic=n3aMatchTopic(question.category,question.q);if(!topic)return '';
  const data=n3aData(currentSubject.id),yearSet=new Set((data.meta.years||[]).map(Number)),hits=n3aFilteredHits(topic.hits,yearSet);
  const sessions=new Set(hits.map(n3aSessionKey)).size,score=n3aTopicScore(hits,yearSet,n3aAllSessionKeys(data,yearSet).size),tier=n3aTier(score);
  return `<button type="button" class="n3a-mini-link" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer" onclick="renderNavi3FrequencyDetail('topic','${topic.id}')"><span style="font-weight:900">필기 연계 · ${escapeHtml(topic.label)}</span><br>${sessions}개 회차 · ${hits.length}문항 · <span class="n3a-score ${tier.cls}" style="margin-top:5px">${tier.label}</span></button>`;
}

// 필기시험 세션 상태
let pastQueue=[], pastIdx=0, pastAnswers=[], pastMode='study', pastStartedAt=0;
let pastSubjectId=null, pastYear=null, pastSession=null;
let pastBaseShort=null;            // v4.34: 현재 선택된 base 코드(navi3 등)
let pastVariant='all';             // v4.34: 'all'|'상선'|'어선'
let pastYearPickVariant='all';     // v4.44: 년도 선택 화면의 상선/어선 필터 (전 년도 모의/학습용)
let pastAllYears=false;            // v4.44: true면 전 년도 통합 세션 — goBackFromPastSession이 년도 선택으로 복귀
let pastReturnView=null;            // v4.97: 빈출 분석에서 연 기출문제의 복귀 화면
const PAST_MOCK_PER_SUBJECT = 25;  // v4.34: 모의시험 = 선택 과목 × 25문

// ── v4.81: 기본 해설과 사용자 해설을 분리한 레지스트리 ──
// 외부 past-explain-{year}.js의 검수된 기본 해설만 표시합니다.
window.MD_EXPLAIN = window.MD_EXPLAIN || {};
function pastQuestionsMatch(a,b){
  return !!a && !!b &&
    a['문제']===b['문제'] &&
    a['정답']===b['정답'] &&
    JSON.stringify(a['선택지']||[])===JSON.stringify(b['선택지']||[]);
}
function getExplainIds(q){
  const exactId=pqid(q);
  const short=q._short||q._gradeShort||'?';
  const baseShort=baseFromShort(short);
  if(baseShort===short || !q._year) return [exactId];

  // 상선/어선 변종의 문제가 실제로 같을 때만 공통(base) 해설을 재사용합니다.
  const session=q._session||q['회차'];
  const baseExam=window.MD_PAST && window.MD_PAST[`${q._year}-${baseShort}-${session}`];
  const baseQuestion=baseExam && baseExam.questions.find(item=>
    item['과목']===q['과목'] && String(item['번호'])===String(q['번호'])
  );
  if(!pastQuestionsMatch(q,baseQuestion)) return [exactId];
  const baseId=`${q._year}|${baseShort}|${session||'?'}|${q['과목']||'?'}|${q['번호']||'?'}`;
  return exactId===baseId ? [exactId] : [exactId,baseId];
}
function getExplain(q){
  const ids=getExplainIds(q);
  const baseId=ids.find(id=>window.MD_EXPLAIN && window.MD_EXPLAIN[id]);
  const e = baseId && window.MD_EXPLAIN[baseId];
  if(!e) return null;
  // html 필드 단일 형식 우선 지원
  if(e.html){
    return { html: e.html, loading: !!e.loading };
  }
  // 폴백: 기존 분리 필드 형식 (해설/용어/키포인트)
  return {
    해설: e['해설'] || e.explain || '',
    용어: e['용어'] || e.terms || {},
    키포인트: e['키포인트'] || e.keypoints || [],
  };
}
// 기본 해설도 허용 태그/스타일만 남겨 innerHTML 주입을 차단합니다.
function sanitizeExplainHtml(input){
  if(typeof input!=='string') return '';
  const template=document.createElement('template');
  template.innerHTML=input;
  const allowedTags=new Set(['BR','STRONG','B','EM','I','SPAN','MARK','P','DIV','UL','OL','LI','TABLE','THEAD','TBODY','TR','TH','TD','HR','CODE']);
  const allowedStyles=new Set([
    'color','background','background-color','font-weight','font-size','font-style','line-height',
    'padding','padding-top','padding-right','padding-bottom','padding-left',
    'margin','margin-top','margin-right','margin-bottom','margin-left',
    'border','border-top','border-right','border-bottom','border-left','border-color','border-width','border-style','border-radius',
    'text-align','white-space','letter-spacing','text-decoration'
  ]);
  [...template.content.querySelectorAll('*')].forEach(el=>{
    if(!allowedTags.has(el.tagName)){
      el.replaceWith(...el.childNodes);
      return;
    }
    [...el.attributes].forEach(attr=>{
      if(attr.name.toLowerCase()!=='style') el.removeAttribute(attr.name);
    });
    if(el.hasAttribute('style')){
      [...el.style].forEach(prop=>{
        const value=el.style.getPropertyValue(prop);
        if(!allowedStyles.has(prop)||/url\s*\(|expression\s*\(|javascript:|@import/i.test(value)){
          el.style.removeProperty(prop);
        }
      });
      if(!el.getAttribute('style')) el.removeAttribute('style');
    }
  });
  return template.innerHTML;
}

// 해설 HTML 안에서 마크다운 일부(굵게/기울임)만 변환. <br>은 이미 들어있다고 가정.
function renderExplainMarkdown(s){
  if(typeof s !== 'string') return '';
  let html = sanitizeExplainHtml(s).replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  html = html.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');

  var sectionColors = {
    '용어 정리':   {bg:'#EFF6FF', border:'#BFDBFE', color:'#1D4ED8'},
    '정답 해설':   {bg:'#F0FDF4', border:'#BBF7D0', color:'#15803D'},
    '핵심 암기':   {bg:'#FFFBEB', border:'#FDE68A', color:'#B45309'},
    '지문 독해':   {bg:'#EFF6FF', border:'#BFDBFE', color:'#1D4ED8'},
    '보기 해석':   {bg:'#F5F3FF', border:'#DDD6FE', color:'#6D28D9'},
    '왜 정답인가?':{bg:'#F0FDF4', border:'#BBF7D0', color:'#15803D'}
  };

  var sectionPattern = /━━\s*([^━]+?)\s*━━/g;
  var parts = [];
  var lastIndex = 0;
  var match;
  while((match = sectionPattern.exec(html)) !== null){
    if(match.index > lastIndex) parts.push({type:'text', content: html.slice(lastIndex, match.index)});
    parts.push({type:'section', name: match[1].trim()});
    lastIndex = match.index + match[0].length;
  }
  if(lastIndex < html.length) parts.push({type:'text', content: html.slice(lastIndex)});

  var result = '';
  var currentSection = null;
  var currentContent = '';

  function flushSection(){
    if(currentSection === null){
      if(currentContent.trim()) result += '<div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.8;color:#0C4A6E">' + currentContent + '</div>';
    } else {
      var sc = sectionColors[currentSection] || {bg:'#F8FAFC', border:'#E2E8F0', color:'#334155'};
      result += '<div style="margin-bottom:10px;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px solid ' + sc.border + ';overflow:hidden">'
             + '<div style="padding:7px 13px;background:' + sc.border + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:' + sc.color + ';letter-spacing:0.5px">' + currentSection + '</div>'
             + '<div style="padding:11px 13px;background:' + sc.bg + ';font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.9;color:#1E293B">' + currentContent.replace(/^(<br>|\s)+/, '') + '</div>'
             + '</div>';
    }
    currentContent = '';
  }

  for(var i=0; i<parts.length; i++){
    var part = parts[i];
    if(part.type === 'section'){
      flushSection();
      currentSection = part.name;
    } else {
      currentContent += part.content;
    }
  }
  flushSection();
  return sanitizeExplainHtml(result);
}
function renderExplainBlock(q, opts){
  opts = opts || {};
  const e = getExplain(q);
  const compact = !!opts.compact;
  if(!e){
    return `<div style="margin-top:${compact?'8px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'10px':'12px'};background:#F8FAFC;border:1px dashed #CBD5E1;border-radius:10px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:var(--textMuted);text-align:center">해설 준비 중입니다</div>`;
  }
  // html 단일 필드 형식 — 정화 후 마크다운 일부 변환
  if(e.html){
    if(e.loading){
      return `<div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#FEF3C7;border:1px solid #FCD34D;border-radius:10px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#92400E;text-align:center">해설을 불러오는 중입니다</div>`;
    }
    return `
      <div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#F0F9FF;border:1px solid #BAE6FD;border-radius:10px">
        <div style="font-size:${compact?'13px':'14px'};line-height:1.75;color:#0C4A6E">${renderExplainMarkdown(e.html)}</div>
      </div>`;
  }
  // 폴백: 기존 분리 필드 렌더링
  const terms = e['용어'] && typeof e['용어']==='object' ? Object.entries(e['용어']) : [];
  const keys = Array.isArray(e['키포인트']) ? e['키포인트'] : [];
  return `
    <div style="margin-top:${compact?'10px':'14px'};padding:${compact?'12px':'14px'};background:#F0F9FF;border:1px solid #BAE6FD;border-radius:10px">
      ${e['해설']?`<div style="font-size:${compact?'13px':'14px'};line-height:1.7;color:#0C4A6E;margin-bottom:${(terms.length||keys.length)?'10px':'0'};white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(e['해설'])}</div>`:''}
      ${terms.length?`
        <div style="margin-bottom:${keys.length?'10px':'0'}">
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1px;color:#075985;margin-bottom:4px">용어</div>
          ${terms.map(([t,d])=>`<div style="font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#0C4A6E;line-height:1.6"><b>${escapeHtml(t)}</b>: ${escapeHtml(d)}</div>`).join('')}
        </div>`:''}
      ${keys.length?`
        <div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1px;color:#075985;margin-bottom:4px">키포인트</div>
          <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:20px;font-size:${compact?'12px':'13px'};color:#0C4A6E;line-height:1.6">
            ${keys.map(k=>`<li style="margin-bottom:2px">${escapeHtml(k)}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>`:''}
    </div>`;
}
function explainCoverage(){
  // 진행도 계산용: 전체 문제 수 대비 해설 있는 문제 수
  if(!window.MD_PAST) return {done:0, total:0};
  let total=0, done=0;
  Object.values(window.MD_PAST).forEach(p=>{
    p.questions.forEach(q=>{
      total++;
      const normalized={...q,_year:p.meta.year,_session:p.meta.session,_short:p.meta.gradeShort,'회차':p.meta.session};
      const ids=getExplainIds(normalized);
      if(ids.some(id=>window.MD_EXPLAIN && window.MD_EXPLAIN[id])) done++;
    });
  });
  return {done, total};
}

// ── v4.46: 편의성 — 오답 노트 / 북마크 / 풀이 기록 ──
const PAST_WRONGS_KEY = 'md_past_wrongs_v1';
const PAST_MARKS_KEY  = 'md_past_marks_v1';
const PAST_HIST_KEY   = 'md_past_history_v1';
const PAST_WRONGS_MAX = 300;  // 최근 N개만 유지

// 문제 고유 ID: 년도|급코드|회차|과목|번호 — 세션 간 안정
function pqid(q){
  const short = q._short || q._gradeShort || '?';
  return `${q._year||'?'}|${short}|${q['회차']||'?'}|${q['과목']||'?'}|${q['번호']||'?'}`;
}
function pqSnapshot(q){
  return {
    _year: q._year, _short: q._short||q._gradeShort||'', _session: q._session,
    '회차': q['회차'], '과목': q['과목'], '번호': q['번호'],
    '문제': q['문제'], '선택지': q['선택지'], '정답': q['정답'],
  };
}
function normalizePastSavedMap(value){
  if(!value || typeof value!=='object' || Array.isArray(value)) return {map:{},changed:!!value};
  const map={};
  let changed=false;
  for(const [storedId,entry] of Object.entries(value)){
    if(!entry || typeof entry!=='object' || !entry.q){
      map[storedId]=entry;
      continue;
    }
    const exactId=pqid(entry.q);
    const normalized={...entry,id:exactId};
    const previous=map[exactId];
    if(!previous || Number(normalized.at||0)>=Number(previous.at||0)) map[exactId]=normalized;
    if(storedId!==exactId || entry.id!==exactId) changed=true;
  }
  return {map,changed};
}

// ── 오답 노트 ──
function loadPastWrongs(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(PAST_WRONGS_KEY);
    if(raw){
      const normalized=normalizePastSavedMap(JSON.parse(raw));
      if(normalized.changed) savePastWrongs(normalized.map);
      return normalized.map;
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[v4.81] 기출 오답 노트 로드 실패:',e)}
  return {};
}
function savePastWrongs(m){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_WRONGS_KEY,JSON.stringify(m),'기출 오답 노트');
}
function addPastWrong(q, myAns){
  const m = loadPastWrongs();
  const id = pqid(q);
  m[id] = { id, q: pqSnapshot(q), myAns, at: Date.now() };
  // 최대치 초과 시 오래된 것부터 제거
  const keys = Object.keys(m);
  if(keys.length > PAST_WRONGS_MAX){
    keys.sort((a,b)=>m[a].at - m[b].at);
    keys.slice(0, keys.length - PAST_WRONGS_MAX).forEach(k=>delete m[k]);
  }
  savePastWrongs(m);
}
function removePastWrong(id){
  const m = loadPastWrongs(); if(m[id]){ delete m[id]; savePastWrongs(m); }
}
function clearPastWrongs(){
  showConfirm('오답 노트를 모두 비우시겠습니까?',()=>{savePastWrongs({});renderPastWrongsList();});
}

// ── 북마크 ──
function loadPastMarks(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(PAST_MARKS_KEY);
    if(raw){
      const normalized=normalizePastSavedMap(JSON.parse(raw));
      if(normalized.changed) savePastMarks(normalized.map);
      return normalized.map;
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[v4.81] 기출 북마크 로드 실패:',e)}
  return {};
}
function savePastMarks(m){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_MARKS_KEY,JSON.stringify(m),'기출 북마크');
}
function isPastMarked(q){
  const m = loadPastMarks(); return !!m[pqid(q)];
}
function togglePastMark(){
  const q = pastQueue[pastIdx]; if(!q) return;
  const m = loadPastMarks();
  const id = pqid(q);
  if(m[id]) delete m[id];
  else m[id] = { id, q: pqSnapshot(q), at: Date.now() };
  savePastMarks(m);
  renderPastCard();
}
function removePastMark(id){
  const m = loadPastMarks(); if(m[id]){ delete m[id]; savePastMarks(m); }
}

// ── 풀이 기록 ──
function loadPastHistory(){
  try{const r=localStorage.getItem(PAST_HIST_KEY); if(r) return JSON.parse(r);}catch(e){}
  return {};
}
function savePastHistory(h){
  return safeStorageSet(PAST_HIST_KEY,JSON.stringify(h),'기출 풀이 기록');
}
function pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session){ return `${baseShort}|${year}|${session}`; }
function recordPastSession(baseShort, year, session, correct, total, mode){
  if(!baseShort || !year || !session || !total) return;
  const h = loadPastHistory();
  const key = pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session);
  const prev = h[key];
  const rate = correct/total;
  h[key] = {
    correct, total, mode, at: Date.now(),
    best: prev ? Math.max(prev.best||0, rate) : rate,
    tries: (prev?prev.tries:0)+1,
  };
  savePastHistory(h);
}
function getPastSessionHistory(baseShort, year, session){
  return loadPastHistory()[pastHistKey(baseShort, year, session)] || null;
}

    </div>
    <div class="card" style="padding:8px 0">
      ${entries.slice(0,80).map(e=>{
        const q = e.q;
        const optMarkers = ['㉮','㉯','㉰','㉱'];
        return `<div style="padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #F1F5F9">
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px">
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textDim);font-weight:600">${q._year}년 ${q['회차']}회 · ${q['과목']} · Q${q['번호']}</div>
            <button onclick="removePastWrong('${e.id}');renderPastWrongsList()" style="background:transparent;border:none;color:var(--textMuted);font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;padding:0 4px" title="제거" aria-label="제거">${icon('close',18)}</button>

          <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px;line-height:1.5">${escapeHtml(q['문제'])}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#DC2626">내 답: ${e.myAns!==null && e.myAns!=null?optMarkers[e.myAns]+' '+escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][e.myAns])):'(미응답)'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#059669">정답: ${optMarkers[q['정답']]} ${escapeHtml(pastChoiceText(q['선택지'][q['정답']]))}</div>
          ${renderExplainBlock(q,{compact:true})}
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}
      ${entries.length>80?`<div style="padding:12px;font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted);text-align:center">... 외 ${entries.length-80}개</div>`:''}
    </div>
    ` : `
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:40px 20px;background:#F8FAFC">
      <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--textDim);margin-bottom:4px">오답이 없습니다</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--textMuted)">문제를 틀리면 자동으로 모입니다</div>
    </div>`}
  `;
}

// 오답/북마크 전용 세션 시작
async function startPastSpecialSession(kind){
  pastReturnView=null;
  const m = kind==='wrong' ? loadPastWrongs() : loadPastMarks();
  const arr = Object.values(m).map(e => ({...e.q, _source: kind}));
  if(!arr.length){ showToast(kind==='wrong'?'오답이 없습니다.':'북마크된 문제가 없습니다.'); return; }
  renderPastLoading('저장한 문제의 해설을 불러오는 중입니다');
  await ensurePastExplainForYears(arr.map(q=>q._year));
  pastQueue = shuffle(arr);
  pastSubjectId=baseFromShort(pastQueue[0]._short||pastQueue[0]._gradeShort);pastBaseShort=pastSubjectId;
  pastIdx = 0;
  pastAnswers = new Array(pastQueue.length).fill(null);
  pastMode = 'study';
  pastStartedAt = Date.now();
  pastAllYears = true;           // 결과 후 goBackFromPastSession이 홈으로 돌아가도록
  pastYear = null; pastSession = null;
  currentMode = 'past';
  renderPastCard();
}
